    - **10c-2b pre-code design** (2026-10-08, read only; head `20261110_106`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-REFUND-014, BR-REFUND-015, BR-CREDIT-008, BR-CREDIT-014, DL-032, BR-AUTH-010; owner answers 3 and 4 to Q-2026-10-07-41, 2 and 3 to Q-2026-10-07-42, answer 4 to the 10c-1b review record, answer 5 to Q-2026-10-08-52). STOPPED before code for Q-2026-10-08-55.
      - Read-only facts (path search of every function the financial or runtime role may run, latest definitions): (1) a one-call verified return (`app.verify_usd_refund_return`) and its one-call reversal (`app.reverse_refund_correction`) are refused for every Deposit Refund without a Pet Refund review, so for every Settlement Deposit Refund (8d) and every correction Deposit Refund (10c-2a-2), by `app.require_correctable_usd_refund` (P5202) and by the general Refund correction check (23514, migration 91, with no pass for a correction link); no function writes `app.refund_corrections` inside a correction yet, although the DB-SETTLE-005 link `refund_correction_id` exists since 10a. (2) A correction's Credit Refund (a Refund with a `settlement_correction_refund_details` row and a Credit source) can still be returned and the return reversed with one call (the approved tightening, owner answer 4 to the 10c-1b review record, closes this). (3) A return adds value back to its source: the Credit (a Credit Refund) or the Deposit (`app.deposit_balance_events` counts every Refund correction of a Deposit Refund, DB-DER-004). (4) After closure, three paths can move a Deposit's money without a correction: the receipt reversal draft (8r; `deposits.correct` records, `financial.approve` confirms; held back only by an active Deposit application, an active forfeiture, an unresolved Deposit Refund or a pending draft; no closed-Settlement check), the Lease forfeiture draft and the forfeiture reversal draft (the latter planned to run only inside a `FORFEITURE_REVERSAL` correction, 10d and 10h); and the one-call verified return and its reversal of a Pet Refund-review Refund (DL-053) still work after closure with no draft. A Pet Refund-review Refund draft itself needs a signed Lease, so it is stopped after termination; a second Settlement Refund is stopped (8d).
      - Proposed split: **10c-2b-1** the approved tightening and the returned-Refund correction for a correction's Credit Refund (return only, and return with a new Refund); **10c-2b-2** the returned-Refund correction for a Deposit Refund (a Settlement Deposit Refund of 8d and a correction Deposit Refund), with the relaxed 8d check for a linked correction and the outflow tightenings of Q-55; **10c-2b-3** the refund-only form (a later correction that refunds value an earlier return-only correction left, Q-42 answer 3). Owner answer 5 to Q-52: 10c-2b-2 is the first to return value to a Pet Deposit, so it carries the test of the 10c-2a-5 to-pet-damage reversal on that real path.
      - Common shape (each variant, as 10c-2a-3): a recorder under `final_settlements.prepare` and a confirm under `financial.approve` (human approver, READ COMMITTED, the 8e-3 lock order, the stale check, `app.begin_settlement_correction`); the draft names the returned Refund (of this Settlement's Lease: a Settlement Deposit Refund of this Settlement or a Refund of one of its corrections), the returned amount in cents (at most its net value), the returned-at time, the return method reference and evidence (the one-call return's inputs), and optionally a new Refund (method, note; for a Deposit the recipient and agreement, BR-REFUND-015); the confirm writes the `refund_corrections` row (kind `VALUE_CORRECTION`, authorization `financial.approve`) linked first (`refund_correction_id`), through a relaxed general Refund correction check that accepts only a row linked to a correction of that Settlement confirmed in this transaction by its approver; the one-call return and its one-call reversal stay refused for every such Refund, and a return recorded by a correction is never reversed with one call (Q-41 point 4). Variant names proposed: `REFUND_RETURN` (return, with or without a new Refund) and `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` (refund-only form).
      - Open (Q-2026-10-08-55): whether a returned Credit with a new Refund first pays its Customer's open debt on the Lease through the shared step (Q-42 answer 2); how a Credit left by a return-only correction may later leave (only a correction's refund-only form, or also the ordinary approved paths); whether a Deposit return with a new Refund runs the step in the same correction (Q-41 answer 4 with Q-42 answer 3); the two outflow tightenings (the receipt reversal draft of a Lease Deposit after closure; the one-call return and reversal of a Pet Refund-review Refund after closure).
      - Owner answers to Q-2026-10-08-55 (2026-10-08; docs/010): (1) a returned Credit Refund's Credit first pays its own Customer's open debt on this Lease, whether or not the correction pays a new Refund; (2) Credit left by a return-only correction is that Customer's Credit, a later correction may refund it, the ordinary Credit paths stay; (3) a returned Deposit Refund goes back to the Deposit and, with a new Refund, the Deposit value first pays the Lease's open debt in the DL-032 order; (4) names `REFUND_RETURN` and `HELD_VALUE_REFUND`; (5) approved tightening: after closure the receipt reversal draft of a Lease Deposit is refused, a wrong Deposit receipt after closure is fixed only through a correction whose kind this plan names (if none fits: a question first); (6) approved tightening: after closure the one-call return and reversal of a Pet Refund-review Refund are refused, the return goes through the 10c-2b-2 correction and is tested there.
      - 10c-2b-1 as built (migration `20261111_107`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-2b-1-refund-return.md): the tightening in `app.require_correctable_usd_refund` (P5202) and `app.validate_general_refund_correction` (23514, with the relaxed pass `app.settlement_refund_return_allowed`: a `VALUE_CORRECTION` under `financial.approve`, linked to a `REFUND_RETURN` correction of the Refund's own Settlement confirmed in this transaction, by its approver, with the draft's Refund, amount, return date, return method reference and evidence, one return per correction); the recorder and confirm as in the common shape above, the overlay a Credit key with the returned amount (the step's R2 rule makes the Credit pay its Customer's debt first); the new Refund is the Credit's value left after the step, at most the returned amount; `app.settlement_correction_refund_allowed` also counts the correction's own returns; the lock function takes the returned Refund's Credit in its id-ordered passes; after the Refund methods `FOR SHARE` and `app.begin_settlement_correction`, the Refund correction check locks the returned Refund row `FOR UPDATE` at the return's insert (an exception to the "method last" note above, safe because the one-call paths refuse such a Refund before any lock and the confirms of one Settlement are serialized by its draft lock; review round 1). For 11e (independent review round 1): the frontend reversal-context validator (`financialActionContext.ts`) expects `refunds.correct` on the reversed return, so a correction-written return (`financial.approve`) would show "invalid financial response" instead of a clear refusal; word the refusal or hide the action for a correction's Refund; the same for the "Record verified USD return" action (`FinancialRefundWorkspace.tsx`, `refund_return` action context, migration 47), which is still offered for a correction's Refund and an 8d Settlement Deposit Refund and now answers 422 (independent review round 2). For slice 12 (security review round 2, optional defence in depth, as in 10c-1b): when the new Refund goes to the payer, the database does not check the "at most the returned amount" cap (the relaxed check runs only for a non-payer); a deferred check on `settlement_correction_refund_details` could apply the per-correction cap to every Credit Refund with a details row. Only a correction's Credit Refund is accepted; the Deposit Refunds wait for 10c-2b-2 (`app.settlement_refund_return_settlement` is the place to extend). Runs: see the record. Carried into the 10c-2b-2 pre-code design (review round 3 notes, not built in 10c-2b-1): bind the return row's `reason` to the draft's in `app.settlement_refund_return_allowed` (defence in depth; no role can insert such rows); the service turns an aware `returned_at` at the edge of the date range (`astimezone(UTC)` raising `OverflowError`, or a tzinfo raising `ValueError`) into `FinalSettlementInvalidError`, as `refund.py` `_instant` does, with a unit case; a test that a correction's Deposit Refund is refused by the 10c-2b-1 form (or accepted once 10c-2b-2 extends it).
      - Owner answer 5 to Q-55, the correction kind for a Deposit receipt found wrong after closure: a receipt whose Payment bounced or was returned is fixed by `RETURNED_PAYMENT` (10e; it reverses the Deposit applications of a Deposit that Payment funded, the receipt and the Payment, and turns a Settlement Refund already paid from that Deposit into an `EXTRA_CHARGE` to the Deposit owner). A receipt wrong while its Payment is real (meant for Rent, the wrong Deposit, the wrong amount) fits no planned kind: STOPPED before 10c-2b-2 code (Q-2026-10-08-56, recommendation (a): a new kind `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` after 10e; and confirm that the approved refusal may be built now). Owner answer to Q-56 (2026-10-08; docs/010): (a), the new kind `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` right after 10e (sub-slice 10e-2 below); the refusal is built now in 10c-2b-2. Known gap until 10e-2: after closure a Deposit receipt wrong while its Payment is real has no fix (and until 10e a receipt whose Payment bounced); it must be closed before V1-056 ends. Owner answers to the 10c-2b-1 report: docs/010 choice (1) kept as built; choice (6) accepted for now, fix path Q-2026-10-08-57 (before 10h).
      - Split of 10c-2b-2 (2026-10-08; a split needs no owner answer): **10c-2b-2a** (migration `20261112_108`) the `REFUND_RETURN` correction also returns a Final Settlement Deposit Refund (8d) and a correction's Deposit Refund (return only: the amount stays held by the Deposit and no step runs; with a new Refund the step runs and the plan's Deposit Refund step pays the rest, only on the DL-032 path), the approved receipt reversal refusal after closure (recorder and insert check, P5S01, passing only a row linked to a correction of that Settlement), the 10c-2b-1 round-3 notes (reason binding), and the 10c-2a-5 to-pet-damage test on the real path (owner answer 5 to Q-52); **10c-2b-2b** the Pet Refund-review Refund after closure (owner answer 6 to Q-55: the one-call return and reversal refused, the return through `REFUND_RETURN`, tested on a new fixture that pays such a Refund before the Lease closes). STOPPED in 10c-2b-2a before its first run (Q-2026-10-08-58: the lint correction allowance for one cause is used up). Owner answer to Q-58: (a), one text-only fix (done), the changed 10c-2b-1 test accepted; reviewer check: a returned Deposit Refund off the DL-032 path must still have a way to be refunded again. 10c-2b-2a as built: as above; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-2b-2a-deposit-refund-return.md. Owner answers to Q-59 built after review round 1: the Lease forfeiture draft refused after closure at record (before the ground check) and the forfeiture row at insert unless linked (no kind links one yet); the plan check's `hold_deposits` flag (only a return-only Credit `REFUND_RETURN`) lets a DL-032 Deposit keep returned money that no open debt can take (review round 2: pet damage is measured after the Pet Deposit's own step); the receipt reversal recorder only at READ COMMITTED; the committed 10c-2b-1 relaxed test's rows carry the draft's reason. Open, not blocking: Q-2026-10-08-60 (the forfeiture recorder outside READ COMMITTED). Carried into 10c-2b-2b (review round 3): do not push 2a without 2b, because after 108 a return-only correction can leave the Pet Deposit holding money that a one-call reversal of a Pet Refund-review Refund's earlier return could use (2b refuses both one-call paths after closure, tested on that state); the service turns an aware `returned_at` at the edge of the date range into `FinalSettlementInvalidError` (10c-2b-1 round-3 note, as `refund.py` `_instant`), with a unit case; optional: refuse `hold_deposits` with a non-empty `deposits` overlay.

Tested on a new fixture: a DL-053 Refund paid and partly returned with one call before closure, then a Settlement that refunds both Deposits, or (review round 1) a pet noncompliance termination (BR-DEP-022) with both Deposits forfeited and a Settlement with no step. Not built: the optional hold-flag refusal (a committed 10c-2b-2a test uses that overlay; slice 12 list). Open, not blocking: Q-2026-10-09-61 (a returned Pet Refund-review Refund whose Pet Deposit is off the DL-032 path has no way out after closure). Review round 1 notes: the 10c-2b-2a lock-order note ("the one-call paths refuse such a Refund before any lock") now reads, for a Pet Refund-review Refund, that the one-call path takes the Deposit row and guard and then refuses, never the Refund row, in the confirm's order, so no deadlock; a REPEATABLE READ one-call caller whose snapshot predates the closure fails on the Deposit guard (40001, tested). For 11e: the `refund_return` and `refund_correction_reversal` action contexts (migration 47) still offer both actions for a Pet Refund-review Refund after closure, which now answer 409; word or hide them, as for the 422 cases of 10c-2b-1. For the future `RETURN_REVERSAL` (Q-2026-10-08-57): besides the `source_deposit_id` switch, its linked reversal row must pass the 10c-2b-2b P5S01 refusal of the Refund correction check, which accepts only a linked `VALUE_CORRECTION` today. For 10d/10h: the Lease forfeiture reversal draft (migration 84) still has no closed-Settlement check, as planned.
      - **10c-2b-3 pre-code design** (2026-10-09, read only; head `20261113_109`; BR-SETTLE-008, BR-SETTLE-013, BR-CREDIT-006, BR-CREDIT-008, BR-CREDIT-014, BR-REFUND-014, BR-REFUND-015, DL-032, BR-AUTH-010; owner answer 3 to Q-2026-10-07-42, answers 1, 2 and 4 to Q-2026-10-08-55, answer 2 to Q-2026-10-08-59). Variant `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` (migration `20261114_110`): a later correction that refunds value an earlier return-only `REFUND_RETURN` correction left, linked to that correction. Staff record it under `final_settlements.prepare` with a reason, the earlier correction (`held_from`: a confirmed `REFUND_RETURN` of the same Settlement whose draft had no new Refund), the new Refund's method and reference note (required, BR-REFUND-014) and the DL-032 plan; only a `financial.approve` holder confirms it (human, READ COMMITTED, the 8e-3 lock order, the stale digest, `app.begin_settlement_correction`, the shared step with its Customer choice re-check). Credit form (the returned Refund had a Credit source C): what the earlier correction left is its returned amount less the Credit applications from C its own step wrote and less the Refunds from C of other confirmed `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` corrections linked to it (55000 when nothing is left); the overlay names C with nothing added, so the step lets C pay only its own Customer's open debt on this Lease first (owner answer 1 to Q-55); the new Refund is what is left in C after the step, at most what the earlier correction left, to C's Customer (BR-CREDIT-008, as choice (1) of 10c-2b-1); nothing left: no Refund. The relaxed Credit Refund check (`app.settlement_correction_refund_allowed`) counts that left value, for a Customer who did not make the Payment (BR-CREDIT-014). Deposit form (the returned Refund had a Deposit source D): D must be on the DL-032 path (55000 otherwise; Q-2026-10-09-61) and hold money (55000 otherwise); no overlay (the money is already held); the step applies or refunds every DL-032 Deposit balance (BR-SETTLE-008), so the plan's Deposit Refund steps refund the rest with the draft's method and note (one per correction), recipient and agreement as BR-REFUND-015. Both forms: refused at record when the correction would write nothing (55000); the lock function takes C in its id-ordered passes; the source digest covers the earlier correction, C (available value, latest event) or D (held value, latest event, path) and the linked `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` Refunds; the draft payload and the confirm's audit event name the earlier correction (`held_from_settlement_correction_id`), and the correction's DB-SETTLE-005 effects link every record it writes (no new table or column); the result reuses `app.settlement_refund_return_result`. Tests: the Credit form with open debt (C pays its Customer's debt first, the rest refunded, at most what was left; a second one refused), the Deposit form (held Pet Deposit money refunded), refusals (not return-only, another Settlement, a Deposit off the DL-032 path on the BR-DEP-022 fixture, stale, Staff cannot confirm), a unit case for the command and the service, forward-only. Choices for the owner: (1) the left-value rule of the Credit form; (2) the Deposit form refunds every held DL-032 Deposit balance, not only the linked correction's amount, because the step requires it; (3) the link to the earlier correction is in the draft payload and the audit event.
      - 10c-2b-3 as built (migration `20261114_110`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-2b-3-held-value-refund.md; owner answers to Q-2026-10-09-63): as designed, with these differences found by the reviews and the owner answers. What the earlier correction left in a Credit is its return less the Credit applications from that Credit written by its own step and by the linked `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` corrections, and less those corrections' Refunds from it; at the confirm this correction's own records are left out (owner answer 2, kept as built; known limit: value spent through the ordinary Credit paths lowers only the available value). Owner answer 1, option (a): an earlier correction is named only while it still has value left (Deposit: its return less the Deposit applications and Refunds from that Deposit written by the linked held-value corrections, new internal `app.settlement_held_deposit_value`), and the Credit or the Deposit must still hold value (55000, also when the plan has other steps); the source digest holds the value left, so at the confirm a used-up value is stale (P5S02). The record-time "writes nothing" refusal is for the Credit form only and is now a backstop. A Deposit correction's draft records the Deposit's owner; the confirm's audit event names the recipient of the step's Deposit Refund (or the owner when nothing was refunded) and lists every Deposit balance the step settled (`settled_deposits`). Tests: 9 in the new module. Runs: T1 (cause E, 1 correction), U1, P1, U2 (cause G traceability, 1 correction), U3, P2, U4, P3 green; lint cause D and cause F each 1 correction. Reviews: round 1 (owner stop, Q-63), round 2 (1 should-fix, a test), round 3 no should-fix.
      - **10c-2b-4 — the stricter Deposit count of `HELD_VALUE_REFUND`** (owner answer of 2026-10-09 to the Deposit counting reported after 10c-2b-3, Q-2026-10-09-64; approved tightening; before 10c-3b). Pre-code design (2026-10-09, read only; head `20261115_111` dumped in the session scratchpad; BR-SETTLE-008, BR-SETTLE-013, DL-032, BR-AUTH-010). Rule: a held-value correction uses, in each Deposit, at most what its earlier correction left there (`app.settlement_held_deposit_value`), the Lease debt it pays and the Refund together; a Deposit the earlier correction returned nothing to (for a Credit correction, every Deposit) gives it nothing; as built after owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-65 ("at most"), it must use some of that value and may leave the rest held for a later correction that names the same earlier correction, and BR-SETTLE-008 and the DL-032 order hold for the money it uses; every other held DL-032 money stays held until a correction names the earlier correction that returned it. Migration `20261116_112`, exact-text patches through `pg_get_functiondef` (owner, SECURITY DEFINER, settings and grants kept; no new function, table or column; no committed migration changes):
        - `app.settlement_correction_plan_result`: an optional overlay key `held_left` (a Deposit id to a whole amount of cents; 22023 when malformed or together with `hold_deposits` true). With it, after the step loop and in place of the every-balance rule, a Deposit used above its limit is refused (55000, "A held-value correction uses at most what its earlier correction left in each Deposit"), and a correction whose limit reaches held money but uses none of it is refused with the existing message (55000, "Every remaining Deposit balance must be applied or refunded"; as built after owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-65). The other rules stay: DL-032 order, Credit before Deposits, pet damage first, no Deposit Refund while debt the Deposit could pay stays open, the Security Deposit before the remaining Pet Deposit. They now order the corrections: while the Security Deposit holds money, a correction limited to the Pet Deposit cannot use the remaining Pet Deposit (step 5). Added after review round 1 (security SF1) and narrowed after review round 2 (independent SF1): while a Pet Deposit keeps held money this correction may not use and pet damage stays open after the Pet Deposit's step, no Security Deposit step pays pet damage (55000, the existing "The Pet Deposit pays open pet damage before any other use or Refund"), so the Security Deposit never pays pet damage while the Pet Deposit could (owner answer 3 (ii) to Q-2026-10-05-20; the hold rule of 10c-2b-2a has the same guard); the Security Deposit may still pay other debt. Owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-65 (option (b)): "at most", not exactly; a correction must use some of the value its earlier correction left in the Deposit and may leave the rest held for a later correction naming the same earlier correction (a correction that uses none keeps the every-balance message); BR-SETTLE-008 and the DL-032 order stay for every correction. Added tests: the Q-65 case settled in either order (plan check called directly, state carried from one correction to the next) and a real correction that leaves value held, then a later one naming the same correction, with a Refund while the charge stays open refused.
        - `app.record_settlement_held_value_refund_draft`: the stored `plan_overlay` gains `held_left`: `{Deposit: value left}` for the Deposit form, `{}` for the Credit form.
        - `app.post_settlement_correction_plan`: for this variant the step passes the draft's `held_left`; a draft with none passes `{}` (no Deposit money).
        - `app.confirm_settlement_held_value_refund`: a draft whose stored `held_left` differs from the recomputed one is stale (P5S02; a draft recorded under `20261114_110` stays pending); the `settled_deposits` comment is corrected. The source digest already holds the value left and the held money, so no digest change.
        - Service: no change (55000 is a conflict, P5S02 stale through the existing mapping).
        - Changed committed test (owner instruction; name kept): `test_a_credit_return_while_a_deposit_holds_money_is_refunded_with_that_money` showed the old limit (a Credit correction linked to Ec refunded the Pet Deposit's 2,000 that Ed returned, and Ed still showed 2,000 left). It now proves the new refusal: linked to Ec with the Pet Deposit's Refund step it is refused and writes nothing; linked to Ec without it, it is recorded (no longer the every-balance refusal), refunds the Credit's 400 and the Pet Deposit keeps its 2,000; a correction linked to Ed refunds them and Ed then has nothing left. Its other checks stay; its traceability note follows. Read-only search of every backend test: no other test changes its expectations (the module's Deposit-form tests use exactly the value left; their no-plan refusal keeps the every-balance message; the plan-check tests of 10c-2a-2 and 10c-2b-2a pass no `held_left`; the source checks of the step (choice re-check order, no `interval` or `age(`) still hold); the 27 head-revision assertions move to `20261116_112`.
        - Tests (new module `test_settlement_held_deposit_limit_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`): two return-only corrections of the Pet Deposit's Settlement Refund (2,000 and 1,000) and a 1,500 charge; linked to the first, Credit 300, Pet 1,200 to the charge and a Refund of 900 (debt and Refund above 2,000) refused, the old full plan (Refund 1,800) refused, a plan using none of the value refused with the every-balance message and a Pet Refund while the charge stays open refused (BR-SETTLE-008), each writing nothing; Refund 500 confirmed (1,700 used, 300 of the first's value left held); a second correction naming the first refunds the 300 and the first then has nothing left; the second refunds its 1,000 (as built after owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-65). A Credit correction while the charge stays open: its Credit pays the debt first, a Pet Deposit step is refused, without it the debt stays open while the Pet Deposit keeps its money, and the Deposit's own correction then pays the debt and refunds the rest. The overlay guards on the plan check called directly (malformed `held_left`, also above the 9e15 cap, with `hold_deposits`), and after review rounds 1 and 2 the per-Deposit cases there (a limit in one Deposit refuses use of the other; the Security Deposit refunded while the Pet Deposit keeps its money; the Security Deposit refused on open pet damage while the Pet Deposit keeps money, allowed on other debt, and the Pet Deposit's own limit paying the pet damage). The known limit on its own path (a correction naming no earlier correction settles held money; a later return's money then used by a correction naming the first). A draft without `held_left` (inserted as a pre-112 draft) is stale at the confirm and stays pending, in the Deposit and the Credit form. Least privilege and forward-only (only the four functions change, each exactly by its replacements).
        - Owner check, the Credit side (reported, not changed): the Credit form does not count the debt it pays in the same way. Its new Refund is at most what the earlier correction left, but the same correction's own step applications from that Credit are left out of that cap (owner answer 2 to Q-2026-10-09-63, kept as built), so debt paid and Refund together may exceed what the earlier correction left (committed test 1: 1,800 left, 1,500 paid to debt and 500 refunded). Only the applications and Refunds of other corrections linked to the earlier correction lower what it left.
- **10c-3 — a wrong Late-payment Charge after closure** (owner answer to Q-2026-10-07-38, 2026-10-07; after 10c-2): a Late-payment Charge correction linked to the Settlement (DB-SETTLE-005 `late_payment_charge_correction_id`), prepared under `final_settlements.prepare` and confirmed only by `financial.approve`; an unpaid Charge is reduced within its unpaid rest; for a paid Charge the released value follows BR-CREDIT-014, as a credit Adjustment of an Invoice line: the correction reverses, as linked records, what paid the reduced part (newest first), released Payment value becomes Credit of the Customer named on the Charge's Invoice, and a linked Refund pays it to that Customer, also when that Customer did not make the Payment. The Late-payment Charge correction then checks Deposit applications before `P4608` (8a-1 note).
  - **10c-3 pre-code design** (2026-10-09, read only; head `20261114_110`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-LATE-005, BR-CREDIT-014, BR-AUTH-010; owner answer to Q-2026-10-07-38). Split (a split needs no owner answer): **10c-3a** an unpaid Late-payment Charge, **10c-3b** a paid one. What exists: a Late-payment Charge (`late_payment_charges`, its own Customer) is changed only by a Late-payment Charge correction row (`REDUCTION`, `CHARGE_REVERSAL`, `REDUCTION_REVERSAL`); its one-call writer `app.correct_late_payment_charge` is refused after closure by the 8e-1 refusal, which already lets a row linked to a correction of the same Settlement through (10a), and DB-SETTLE-005 already has the link `late_payment_charge_correction_id`; that writer's P4608 check has a deferred twin in the row's validator since migration `20260905_39` (23514 when the result is below the Charge's active applications, Deposit applications included since 8a-1 and not reversed since 10c-2a-1; corrected after 10c-3a review round 2), and the row's serializer orders a correction after every allocation and Credit application on the Charge; the 8a-1 trigger refuses every Charge correction row while a Deposit application on that Charge stands (P4603).
    - **10c-3a** (migration `20261115_111`): the `CHARGE_REDUCTION` variant also takes a Late-payment Charge of the Settlement's Lease (Q-38 recommendation (a), owner answer yes), as new functions beside the Invoice-line ones: recorder `app.record_settlement_late_charge_reduction_draft` (`final_settlements.prepare`; the draft payload has `target_kind` `LATE_PAYMENT_CHARGE` and `reduction` {`late_payment_charge_id`, `amount_usd_cents`}) and confirm `app.confirm_settlement_late_charge_reduction` (`financial.approve`, human approver, READ COMMITTED, stale P5S02, replay); the Invoice-line confirm is patched to refuse a draft with a `target_kind` (not found), so neither confirm takes the other's draft. Refusals at record and again at the confirm, the same rules as the Invoice-line form: an open Settlement 55000; a Charge not of an Invoice of the Settlement's Lease P0002; a Charge on a VOID Invoice or a reversed (terminal) Charge 55000; a Charge a Deposit application paid (8a-1, as the line form; the paid case is 10c-3b) 55000; more than the unpaid rest (the current obligation less the active applications) 55000 "The charge reduction would release paid value". The confirm locks, in the 8e-3 order, the draft, the Settlement's draft lock, the Lease row FOR UPDATE, then the Charge row and its Invoice FOR UPDATE (the order of `app.lock_payment_allocation_target`, so a Payment or Credit on the Charge commits before or waits), checks the source digest (the Settlement, the Charge's current obligation, corrections, active applications and Invoice state), runs `app.begin_settlement_correction`, then links and writes one Late-payment Charge correction: `REDUCTION` of the amount, or `CHARGE_REVERSAL` when the amount is the whole current obligation (possible only with no active applications; the row's validator requires a reversal to remove all of it), authorization `financial.approve`, the draft's reason, correlation the correction, stamped after the latest correction of the Charge; audit `late_payment.charge_corrected` (before and after snapshots) and `final_settlement.correction.confirmed`. The service gains `RecordLateChargeReductionCorrectionCommand`, `record_late_charge_reduction` and `confirm_late_charge_reduction`. Tests (new module): a partial `REDUCTION` and a whole-Charge `CHARGE_REVERSAL` on an unpaid Charge, then a further one refused (terminal); a Charge partly paid after closure reduced only within its unpaid rest (more refused, nothing written); the Deposit-paid refusal on a really closed two-Signer Settlement; another Lease's Charge P0002; Staff cannot confirm; stale draft stays pending; replay; the database guards; the Invoice-line confirm refuses a Charge draft and the Charge confirm an Invoice-line draft; ledger reconciles; least privilege and forward-only. Routes stay in 11e.
    - 10c-3a as built (migration `20261115_111`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-3a-late-charge-reduction.md): as designed. Review round 1 added the lock tests (a stale draft waits for the Lease row and the Charge row), the replay-path tests of the two confirms and a no-time-limit source check; the Charge form counts only Deposit applications not reversed; the accepted until-10h gap covers a Charge too (10h test). Runs: T1, U1 3663, P1 1988, T2, U2 3663 green; lint causes H (2 of 2) and J, type cause I, 1 correction each. Reviews: round 1 2 should-fix (tests), round 2 no should-fix. Slice 12: a test of choice (3); the stamp after the Charge's latest allocation and Credit application.
    - **10c-3b** (after 10c-3a; pre-code design then): the `REFUND` variant also takes a paid Late-payment Charge (Q-38 (b)): the correction reverses, as linked records, what paid the reduced part, newest first across allocations, Credit applications and Deposit applications (Q-41 answer 2), writes the Charge correction, makes released Payment value the Charge Customer's Credit (BR-CREDIT-014 as amended), runs the shared DL-032 step (released Credit first pays its own Customer's open debt, Q-42 answer 2; released Deposit value in the DL-032 order) and refunds the rest. Points to settle in its design: the 8a-1 trigger refuses a Charge correction while a Deposit application the newest-first release does not reach stays on the Charge (the Q-2026-10-07-46 case for Adjustments); and where the released Payment value's BR-CREDIT-014 Credit is recorded, since the existing release record (`invoice_credit_excess_releases`) needs an Adjustment. Its linked reversals must be written before the Charge correction row's deferred validator runs (it refuses a result below the active applications), and the correction's stamp must follow the Charge's latest allocation and Credit application too (the serializer; 10c-3a security review round 2, note 3).
    - **10c-3b pre-code design** (2026-10-09, read only; head `20261116_112`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-LATE-005, BR-CREDIT-006, BR-CREDIT-008, BR-CREDIT-014, BR-REFUND-014, BR-SETTLE-008, DL-032, BR-AUTH-010; owner answers to Q-2026-10-07-38 (b), Q-2026-10-07-41 answer 2, Q-2026-10-07-42 answers 1 and 2, Q-2026-10-07-46, Q-2026-10-09-66 and answer 4 to Q-2026-10-09-65). Split (owner answer 4 to Q-2026-10-09-65): **10c-3b-1** a paid Charge whose paid part was paid by Payment allocations and Credit applications; **10c-3b-2** a Charge a Deposit application paid. Both are the `REFUND` variant with draft `target_kind` `LATE_PAYMENT_CHARGE`, as 10c-3a did for `CHARGE_REDUCTION`: new recorder and confirm beside the Invoice-line ones, and the Invoice-line `REFUND` confirm and its result reader patched to refuse a draft with a `target_kind` (not found), so neither confirm takes the other's draft.
      - **10c-3b-1** (migration `20261117_113`):
        - Release record (owner answer 2 to Q-2026-10-09-66, choice accepted in owner answer 4 to Q-2026-10-09-65): `app.invoice_credit_excess_releases` is relaxed, never rebuilt. `invoice_adjustment_id` becomes nullable. A new nullable `late_payment_charge_correction_id` (FK to `late_payment_charge_corrections`, ON DELETE RESTRICT, indexed) is added. A new check requires exactly one of the two links (`num_nonnulls(...)=1`; every existing row has its Adjustment). The fixed reason check becomes "Invoice adjustment credit excess" with an Adjustment link, or "Late-payment Charge correction credit excess" with a Charge correction link. The table stays append-only with no role grant. Every money movement links to the correction: the reversals, the re-posted rests and the Refunds are DB-SETTLE-005 effects; the Charge correction is an effect (`late_payment_charge_correction_id`); each release row names that Charge correction; and the released Credit names its release row (`customer_credit_id`), as in 10c-1b, where the chain runs through the linked Adjustment.
        - Patched readers (exact-text patches; owner, SECURITY DEFINER, settings and grants kept). `app.validate_invoice_credit_excess_release` gets a Charge branch: the reversed allocation or Credit application must target that Charge and carry the same reason, and the release must be at most what it applied. `app.validate_credit_recognition` finds the Customer through the Charge correction (the Charge's Customer, BR-CREDIT-014), as it finds the Invoice Customer through the Adjustment. `app.settlement_correction_refund_allowed` and `app.settlement_correction_credit_id` join the release rows through the correction's Charge correction effect as well as its Adjustment effect. The two audit labels of re-posted allocations and Credit applications use the release row's reason. Readers that already key on the released source, its replacement or the Credit stay unchanged: the 8f and 8g basis (`app.credit_customer_refund_basis`), the moved-Credit and wrong-Unit refusals, the Settlement Credit application remainder refusal and the proposal check.
        - Recorder `app.record_settlement_late_charge_refund_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, refund, reason, authorization)` (`final_settlements.prepare`; input {`late_payment_charge_id`, `amount_usd_cents`, `refund_method_id`, `reference_note`, `plan`}; the draft stores the Charge's source Invoice too):
          - refusals as the 10c-3a Charge form: an open Settlement 55000; a Charge not of the Settlement's Lease P0002; a VOID Invoice or a reversed Charge 55000; more than the current obligation 55000; an amount that releases no paid value 55000 (that is a `CHARGE_REDUCTION`);
          - an active, not reversed Deposit application on the Charge: 55000 until 10c-3b-2;
          - the released sources: whole Payment allocations and Credit applications on the Charge, newest first, until the paid part is covered (Q-41 answer 2);
          - the overlay names the released value as Credit (`credits`, released Payment value keyed by its allocation and given to the Charge's Customer, released Credit value back to its own Credit) and the Charge's corrected rest as the obligation;
          - the shared DL-032 step's plan check, with the Customer choice and one Refund method and note per correction (as 10c-1b and 10c-2a-3);
          - the source digest: the Settlement, the Charge (current obligation, corrections, active applications, VOID state), the released sources and their Credits.
        - Confirm `app.confirm_settlement_late_charge_refund(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; human approver; READ COMMITTED; replay; stale P5S02 and the draft stays pending):
          - locks: the 8e-3 order (the draft lock, the Settlement's draft lock, the Lease row FOR UPDATE), then the lock function. The lock function is patched so that a Charge `REFUND` draft adds its Charge to the Charge pass FOR UPDATE; its released sources' Payments and Credits already join the id-ordered passes through `released_sources`, and its source Invoice joins the Invoice pass through the `refund` object. Then the Refund method FOR SHARE, then `app.begin_settlement_correction`.
          - Phase 1: each released source reversed whole, newest first, linked (`payment_allocation_reversal_id`, `credit_application_reversal_id`), with the Charge reason. The reversals are stamped after the Charge's latest event (`app.allocation_target_latest_at('LATE_PAYMENT_CHARGE', ...)`) and the Credit's latest event.
          - Then one linked Charge correction: `REDUCTION` of the amount, or `CHARGE_REVERSAL` when it is the whole current obligation (the 10c-3a rule). Authorization `financial.approve`, the draft's reason, correlation the correction. Stamped after the reversals and after every allocation and Credit application on the Charge, so it passes the serializer (10c-3a security note 3).
          - Phase 2: each reversed source's unreleased rest is re-posted to the same Charge (an allocation or Credit application with the same basis, one `LATE_PAYMENT_CHARGE` detail), linked, with its release row. Released Payment value becomes a new Credit of the Charge's Customer (BR-CREDIT-014, also when that Customer did not pay). Released Credit value returns to its Credit.
          - Then the shared step (released Credit first pays its own Customer's open debt on the Lease, Q-42 answer 2). Then one Credit Refund per released source of what is left in its Credit, at most what was released to it, to that Credit's Customer, each with its `settlement_correction_refund_details` row (method and note, BR-REFUND-014).
          - `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`: the Charge correction's deferred validator refuses a result below the active applications (23514), and every linked record is written by then.
          - Audit: `late_payment.charge_corrected` (before and after) and `final_settlement.correction.confirmed` (released sources, re-posts, released Credits, step records, Refunds).
        - Service: `RecordLateChargeRefundCorrectionCommand`, `SettlementCorrectionService.record_late_charge_refund` and `confirm_late_charge_refund` (the existing error mapping). Routes stay in 11e.
        - Tests (new module `test_settlement_late_charge_refund_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`), on really closed Settlements:
          - a Charge paid by the Customer's Payment after closure: part refunded, the rest re-posted, the Charge reduced, a Refund of the released value;
          - the same Customer with other open debt on the Lease: the released Credit pays it first and only the rest is refunded;
          - a third-party payer: the released Credit and its Refund go to the Charge's Customer, not the payer, and the payer's ledger is unchanged; the one-call Credit reversal of that Credit is refused (8g);
          - a Charge paid by a Credit application: the value returns to that Credit;
          - the whole obligation released: `CHARGE_REVERSAL`, then a further correction refused;
          - refusals: unpaid only (a `CHARGE_REDUCTION`), another Lease P0002, a Deposit-paid Charge 55000, Staff cannot confirm, stale stays pending, replay, the database guards;
          - the relaxed table: exactly one link, the reason pairs, append-only, an unlinked release refused;
          - the Invoice-line and Charge confirms refuse each other's drafts;
          - ledger reconciliation; least privilege and forward-only (only the listed functions and the table change).
      - 10c-3b-1 as built (migration `20261117_113`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-3b-1-late-charge-refund.md): as designed, with these differences. (1) Owner approval in the session (Q-2026-10-09-68): one more patched function (nine in all), the shared plan check `app.settlement_correction_plan_result`, whose Credit overlay now also accepts a released allocation on a Late-payment Charge of the Lease as Credit of the Charge's Customer (it joined `allocation.invoice_id`, which a Charge allocation does not have). (2) "Its result reader" is read as the confirm's replay path (as 10c-3a): `app.settlement_refund_result` itself is unchanged; the Invoice-line confirm refuses a draft with a `target_kind` on the replay path and the main path. (3) The table keeps its existing read grant to the runtime and financial roles (the design's "no role grant" means no new grant; no role writes it). (4) The new FK and index have explicit names (`invoice_credit_excess_release_charge_correction_fkey`, `invoice_credit_excess_release_charge_correction_idx`), because the default names pass 63 characters. (5) New internal helpers: `app.settlement_late_charge_refund_input`, `_sources`, `_source_state`, `_overlay`, `_plan`, `_result` and `app.settlement_late_charge_instant` (the stamp after the Charge's and the Credit's latest event). (6) The tests use the two-Signer overdue Lease of 8c, really closed (the remaining Pet Deposit pays Rent and the Charge stays unpaid at closure, or, for the Settlement Credit application case, the Primary Tenant's Credit pays 1,500 of it); the other-Lease Charge comes from the 8e-1 overdue fixture. (7) The third-party test shows that the Refund is not on the payer's ledger, as the committed 10c-1b test does; the released Credit is on it, because the payer's ledger lists the Credits of the payer's Payment. (8) For 10c-3b-2 (security review round 1, note 3): the plan function keeps no `application_usd_cents` for a `DEPOSIT_APPLICATION` source (the Invoice-line plan does), so the overlay would read none, and Phase 1 treats every source that is not an allocation as a Credit application; both fail closed today, because a Deposit-paid Charge is refused, and 10c-3b-2 must add both.
      - **10c-3b-2** (after 10c-3b-1; its own pre-code check then): the Charge `REFUND` also releases Deposit applications on the Charge (newest first with the other sources, Q-41 answer 2), as the 10c-2a-1 Deposit application reversals linked to the correction. Owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-66 and the Q-2026-10-07-46 answer, as for Adjustments: the 8a-1 refusal of a Charge correction while a Deposit application stands (`app.refuse_deposit_applied_obligation_change`, P4603) lets through only a Charge correction linked to a correction of that Charge's own Settlement, confirmed in the same transaction by that correction's approver; every unlinked Charge correction stays refused. It fits a Charge: the release stays newest first; the Charge correction's deferred validator already refuses a result below the active applications, Deposit applications not reversed included (no Charge is paid more than its new amount); released Deposit value is freed only by the correction's own Deposit application reversal and never becomes Customer Credit; the overlay adds it to its Deposit, and the shared step applies or refunds it in the DL-032 order. Tests: an older Deposit application on the Charge that the release does not reach stays, and the reduction passes; an unlinked Charge correction on a Deposit-paid Charge stays refused; a mixed Charge (a Payment newer than a Deposit application).
      - **10c-3b-2 pre-code check** (2026-10-09, read only; head `20261117_113`; owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-09-66 and the Q-2026-10-07-46 answer; no owner question: the Q-46 answer fits a Charge, as the Q-66 findings said). Migration `20261118_114`:
        - The 8a-1 refusal (`app.refuse_deposit_applied_obligation_change`, its `late_payment_charge_corrections` branch, P4603) is patched (exact text, relaxing) to let through only a Charge correction linked to a correction of that Charge's own Settlement (the Settlement of its source Invoice's Lease), confirmed in this transaction by that correction's approver under `financial.approve`: a new internal `app.settlement_correction_charge_correction_allowed(charge, correction row, actor)`, the twin of `app.settlement_correction_adjustment_allowed` (the effect's `late_payment_charge_correction_id`, the decision's transaction id and `decided_at` bound). Every unlinked Charge correction stays refused (P4603, and after closure first P5S01).
        - `app.settlement_late_charge_refund_plan` (exact text): the Deposit refusal is removed; a `DEPOSIT_APPLICATION` source keeps `application_usd_cents` (the whole application) beside `released_usd_cents`, as the Invoice-line plan does (plan difference 8), so the overlay adds the whole application to its Deposit and leaves the unreleased rest as the Charge's obligation for the step to pay again.
        - `app.confirm_settlement_late_charge_refund` (exact text): Phase 1 gains the `DEPOSIT_APPLICATION` branch of the Invoice-line confirm (a linked `deposit_application_reversal_id`, the draft's reason, `financial.approve`, stamped after the Charge's latest event and the Deposit's latest balance event, `app.deposit_balance_latest_at`), and the Credit-application branch names its kind (plan difference 8); Phase 2 is unchanged (a Deposit's rest is paid again only by the plan's step, never as Credit); the confirmed audit lists the Deposit application reversals. The lock function already takes every Deposit of the Lease (draft lock and row) in its last pass.
        - Q-46 conditions on a Charge: the release stays newest first across all three kinds; an older Deposit application the release does not reach stays and still pays the Charge; the Charge correction's deferred validator already refuses a result below the active applications, Deposit applications not reversed included, so no Charge is paid more than its new amount; Deposit value is freed only by the correction's own reversal and goes back to its Deposit, never to Customer Credit; the shared step then applies or refunds it in the DL-032 order (BR-SETTLE-008).
        - Tests (new module `test_settlement_late_charge_deposit_refund_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`), on the really closed two-Signer Settlement whose remaining Pet Deposit paid the Charge: a Refund of part of the Charge reverses the Pet Deposit application whole, reduces the Charge and the step pays the rest of the Charge and the Rent again from the Pet Deposit (no Refund while Rent stays open); an older Deposit application the release does not reach stays (the Pet Deposit pays part of the Charge at closure, a Payment the rest after closure, a Refund releases only the Payment) and the reduction passes 8a-1 only because it is linked; a mixed Charge (a Payment newer than a Deposit application) released across both, the released Payment Credit first paying its Customer's own debt (Q-42) before the Deposit value; an unlinked Charge correction on a Deposit-paid Charge stays refused (P5S01 after closure; the new allow function answers false outside its correction); the 10c-3a `CHARGE_REDUCTION` still refuses a Deposit-paid Charge; ledger reconciliation; least privilege and forward-only (only the listed functions change).
      - 10c-3b-2 as built (migration `20261118_114`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10c-3b-2-late-charge-deposit-refund.md): as designed, with these differences. (1) The test module has five tests: the three listed cases (the whole release with the Pet Deposit paying again, the older Deposit application that stays, the mixed Charge with Credit first), a hand-built test inside one correction that shows the allow function's true answer for the approver's linked Charge correction and the Charge correction's own deferred check refusing a Charge paid more than its new amount (another actor stays P4603), and least privilege with forward-only; the 10c-3a `CHARGE_REDUCTION` refusal and the unlinked one-call refusal (P5S01) are in the second test. (2) The committed 10c-3b-1 test `test_the_refund_is_refused_for_unpaid_too_large_deposit_paid_or_other_lease_charges` changed one expectation (its refusal now comes from the shared step, "Every remaining Deposit balance must be applied or refunded"; docs/010). (3) The plan patch also replaced the 10c-3b-1 refusal comment; the confirm patch replaced the comment that said a Deposit application is never a source and extended the 8e-1 comment of the Charge correction to the 8a-1 refusal.
      - Not changed: the 10c-3a `CHARGE_REDUCTION` Charge form (it still refuses a Deposit-paid Charge); the one-call writer `app.correct_late_payment_charge` (refused after closure). Known gap until 10h (as for 10c-3a): a one-call reversal of an allocation that paid the Charge at closure.
- **10d — `FORFEITURE_REVERSAL`** (owner answer 4): Deposit applications linked to the correction (DL-032), then the Deposit Refund of the rest.
  - Owner answer 5 to Q-2026-10-08-52 (also for 10c-2b): whichever of 10c-2b and 10d first returns value to a Pet Deposit adds a test of the 10c-2a-5 to-pet-damage reversal (a line's Security Deposit application reversed while a Pet Deposit on the DL-032 path holds money, and the Pet Deposit then paying the line first as pet damage) on that real path, not a hand-built state.
  - **10d pre-code design** (2026-10-10, read only; head `20261118_114`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-008, BR-DEP-019, BR-DEP-023, BR-DEP-026, DL-032, BR-REFUND-014, BR-REFUND-015, BR-AUTH-010; owner answer 4 to Q-2026-10-06-36, answer 1 to Q-2026-10-06-37, answers 1 and 3 to Q-2026-10-07-41, answers 1 to 3 to Q-2026-10-07-42, Q-2026-10-05-24). Migration `20261119_115`. No owner question: the rules give every step; three points for the owner's information are at the end.
    - What exists: a Lease forfeiture (`deposit_dispositions` with `governing_lease_id` and `amount_usd_cents`) is reversed only by one `deposit_disposition_reversals` row (unique per disposition; reason, evidence reference, actor, authorization reference), written by `app.reverse_deposit_disposition` (migration 27: it locks the disposition, then the Deposit, and checks the Property and a Lease disposition) through `app.reverse_deposit_disposition_v2` (command record and audit `deposit.disposition.reversed`). Its only caller is the 6b draft confirm `app.confirm_lease_deposit_reversal_draft` (migration 84: the `lease-deposit-forfeiture:` lock and the Deposit row first, authorization `deposits.correct`, the approver as actor). The preview (migration 89) puts a Deposit whose every confirmed forfeiture is reversed on the DL-032 path (Q-24), and the shared step's Deposit Refund check needs that path when the Refund is written. DB-SETTLE-005 already has the link `deposit_disposition_reversal_id` (migration 97). After closure a new forfeiture is refused unless linked (10c-2b-2a) and no correction links one (owner, 2026-10-09); a reversal after closure is still possible through the 6b draft, without the step (the known gap closed in 10h).
    - Recorder `app.record_settlement_forfeiture_reversal_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, reversal, reason, authorization)` (`final_settlements.prepare`; EXECUTE to the financial role): input {`deposit_disposition_id`, `evidence_reference` (1 to 500 characters), `refund_method_id`, `reference_note`, `plan`}; the reason at most 500 characters. Refusals: an open Settlement 55000; a disposition that is not a confirmed forfeiture of a Deposit of the Settlement's Lease (a Reservation disposition included) P0002; a forfeiture already reversed 55000; an inactive Refund method 55000; one Refund method, note, recipient and agreement per correction (22023, as 10c-1b); the remaining Pet Deposit choice rules (Q-44 answer 3, 10c-2a-6). The overlay gives the Deposit the forfeited amount (`deposits`: the disposition's `amount_usd_cents`; no obligations, no Credits), and the shared plan check runs on it (the check adds the overlay to the preview's held value whatever the path, so the kept Deposit is usable in the check; at the confirm the real reversal puts it on the DL-032 path, and the remaining Pet Deposit's `chosen_by` comes from the termination ground, so both checks agree). Source digest: the Settlement, the disposition and its reversal state, the Deposit's held value and latest balance event, and the pending drafts of the Deposit.
    - Confirm `app.confirm_settlement_forfeiture_reversal(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; human approver; READ COMMITTED; replay; stale P5S02, the draft stays pending). Locks in the 8e-3 order: the draft lock, the Settlement's draft lock, the Lease row FOR UPDATE, the lock function (unchanged: its last pass takes every Deposit of the Lease with its `lease-deposit-forfeiture:` lock and row), then the disposition row FOR UPDATE (the Deposit before the disposition, as the 6b confirm), then the Refund method FOR SHARE. Then `app.begin_settlement_correction`, the link `deposit_disposition_reversal_id`, and the reversal written through `app.reverse_deposit_disposition_v2` as the 6b confirm writes it (every earlier reversal rule applies: the approver as actor, `deposits.correct`, the draft's reason and evidence reference, the command record and the `deposit.disposition.reversed` audit; the correction's draft id as the command key); then the shared step (`app.post_settlement_correction_plan`: Credit first, Deposit applications in the DL-032 order, the Deposit Refund of the rest with the draft's method and note), `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`, and the audit `final_settlement.correction.confirmed` (the disposition, the reversal, the step's records). No patch is expected; if the reversal's stamp (`statement_timestamp()` in migration 27) or another check needs one, it is an exact-text relaxing patch named in the as-built line.
    - Service: `RecordForfeitureReversalCorrectionCommand`, `SettlementCorrectionService.record_forfeiture_reversal` and `confirm_forfeiture_reversal` (the existing error mapping). Routes stay in 11e.
    - Tests (new module `test_settlement_forfeiture_reversal_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`), on a really closed Settlement whose Security Deposit was kept (forfeiture confirmed before closure): with no open debt the whole Deposit is refunded to the Primary Tenant (and, with the written agreement, to another signed Signer); with open debt the Deposit pays it first in the DL-032 order and only the rest is refunded, or nothing is refunded when the debt takes all; a Customer with Credit pays that Customer's own debt first (Q-42 answer 1); a plan that leaves money unused or refunds while debt stays open is refused (BR-SETTLE-008); refusals (an open Settlement, another Lease's or a Reservation disposition P0002, a reversed forfeiture, a pending 6b reversal or forfeiture draft of the Deposit, Staff cannot confirm, a stale draft stays pending, replay, the database guards); the reversal row, its link and the Deposit back on the DL-032 path in the preview; the 6b draft of the same forfeiture refused afterwards (one reversal per disposition); ledger reconciliation; least privilege and forward-only.
    - For the owner (information, not blocking): (1) the reversal refunds what the debt does not take in the same correction (owner answer 4, BR-SETTLE-008); unlike the return-only Refund correction, there is no hold of the reversed money while the Customer cannot be reached; (2) on a kept Pet Deposit that also holds returned money of a Pet Refund-review Refund (Q-2026-10-09-61, open), the reversal puts all of it on the DL-032 path, so that money is also paid to debt or refunded (the step settles every held balance); Q-61 still decides the case without a reversal; (3) until 10h the 6b draft still reverses a closed Settlement's forfeiture without the step (the plan's known gap).
    - 10d as built (migration `20261119_115`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10d-forfeiture-reversal.md): as designed (owner accepted the design and its three points, Q-2026-10-10-69 answer 6), with these differences. (1) Owner answer 2 to Q-2026-10-10-71: the recorder refuses (55000) a reversal while another forfeiture of the same Deposit stays active; at the confirm the digest's list of other active forfeitures answers first (P5S02). (2) Owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-10-71: the recipient rules are tested by refusals only (a Customer who is not a Signer, an agreement for the Primary Tenant); the positive agreed-Signer Refund on a kept Deposit is a task before 10h. (3) The digest leaves correction drafts out of the Deposit's waiting drafts (the draft itself is pending at its confirm). (4) A new internal result reader `app.settlement_forfeiture_reversal_result` adds the linked reversal to `app.settlement_refund_return_result`. (5) A pending forfeiture draft of the Deposit is not tested: none can be recorded after closure (10c-2b-2a); a waiting 6b reversal draft is tested (refused at record, stale at the confirm). No patch of a committed function.
- **10e — `RETURNED_PAYMENT`**, with and without a closed Settlement (owner item; owner answer 5), with the tests the owner asked for: a bounced Payment whose Credit or whose Deposit a Settlement used, and a returned third-party Payment whose adjustment Credit was released to another Customer. Uses the 10c reversal records.
  - **10e pre-code design** (2026-10-10, read only; head `20261119_115`, read from a throwaway database's catalog; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-016, BR-PAY-042, BR-CREDIT-014, BR-AUTH-010; owner answers 1 and 5 to Q-2026-10-06-36, answers 2, 3 and 5 to Q-2026-10-06-37, answer 5 to Q-2026-10-08-55, answer 1 to Q-2026-10-08-56; DB-PAY-006, DB-PAY-009, DB-SETTLE-005). Open points: Q-2026-10-10-72 (blocks 10e-1b and 10e-1c, not 10e-1a).
    - What exists: (1) a Payment reversal (DB-PAY-006, `app.validate_payment_reversal`) needs `app.payment_disposition_summary` all zero (no active allocation, no Credit recognized from the Payment that is not reversed, no active Deposit receipt, every Refund of the Payment fully returned) and is dated after every dependent event; the one-call `app.reverse_payment_command` (`payments.reverse`) only inserts that row, so staff must first undo every use one call at a time. (2) What a returned Payment must undo, each with its own guard: allocations (a `WRONG_UNIT_MOVE` allocation refused, P4904, slice 7); Credit applications (a Final Settlement Credit application refused unless linked, P5S01, 8e-2; a re-posted remainder refused, 10c-1b); Credits (the reversal needs the whole Credit available, 23514; refused for a Credit released from a moved allocation that is not the payer's, P4904, slice 7, and for a Credit released to a Customer other than the payer, P4904, 8g); Deposit receipts (`app.validate_deposit_receipt_reversal`: refused after closure unless linked, P5S01, and while the Deposit has an active forfeiture, a Refund not fully returned, an active Deposit application or a pending Refund, forfeiture or Settlement draft, 55000; no one-call path since 8r); Deposit applications (written and reversed only inside a correction, 10c-2a-1). (3) Each reversal table checks a fixed authorization string (`payments.allocations.reverse`, `credits.applications.reverse`, `credits.reverse`); the 10c writers write them with the approver as actor (as 10d writes `deposits.correct`; the slice 12 approver-naming item). (4) DB-SETTLE-005 already has every link a returned Payment needs, and `app.settlement_correction_linked` knows each. (5) The only Deposit receipt writer an application role may run is `app.create_reservation` (one receipt: the Reservation's Security Deposit), so today every Deposit a Payment funded has exactly that receipt. (6) The `financial_drafts` CHECKs list the kinds, the preparer capabilities and their pairs, and a WITHDRAWN decision checks the preparer list (migration 84); `payments.reverse` is in none of them. (7) A Refund paid from the returned value (a Payment Refund, a Refund of one of its Credits, a Refund of a Deposit it funded) blocks the Payment, Credit or receipt reversal until it is fully returned; nothing balances it otherwise (Q-72 point 1).
    - **10e-1a — `PAYMENT_RETURN` when no closed Settlement relied on the Payment's records** (migration `20261120_116`; decided by owner answers 2, 3 and 5 to Q-2026-10-06-37 and DB-PAY-009):
      - Table `app.payment_returns` (DB-PAY-009): `id`, `property_id`, `payment_id` (unique), `draft_id` (unique, FK `financial_drafts`), `settlement_correction_id` (nullable, unique, FK; written from 10e-1b), `reason`, `occurred_at`, `actor_id`, `authorization_reference` (`financial.approve`). Append-only; no role may read or write it. BEFORE INSERT: the Payment is RECEIVED and of the Property; the draft is a `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft naming this Payment with the same reason (from 10e-1b also a `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction draft); the actor is an active financial actor; `occurred_at` not later than the clock plus 5 seconds. Deferred check at commit: the draft is CONFIRMED by that actor in this transaction (the decision's transaction id and `decided_at` test of 10a) and the Payment is REVERSED. The evidence reference stays in the draft payload (no new column).
      - Relaxing draft CHECKs: kind `PAYMENT_RETURN`, preparer `payments.reverse`, the pair, and WITHDRAWN by `payments.reverse`. The build checks every function that lists draft kinds or preparer capabilities (read-only search, as in 6b).
      - `app.payment_return_in_transaction(payment)` (internal, never NULL): true only when a `payment_returns` row of that Payment exists whose draft was CONFIRMED in this transaction (the same test as `app.settlement_correction_linked`). Relaxing patches, exact text: the slice 7 refusals (`app.validate_wrong_unit_move_reversal`, `app.validate_moved_credit_reversal`) and the 8g refusal (`app.validate_credit_customer_credit_reversal`) let a reversal of that Payment's record through when it is true. Not patched: the 8e-2 refusal and the receipt reversal's closure check (a closed Settlement's records pass only through a linked correction, 10e-1b).
      - Recorder `app.record_payment_return_draft(property, actor, draft, payment, input {evidence_reference 1 to 500 characters}, reason, authorization)` (`payments.reverse`; EXECUTE to the financial role): the Payment of the Property (P4901 otherwise) and RECEIVED (55000); the payload lists every active allocation (target, Customer, amount), every Credit recognized from the Payment (Customer, amount) with its active applications, and every active Deposit receipt (Deposit, amount), so the approver sees which debt comes back and on whose ledger. Refused (55000, a conflict, the message naming the case): a record a closed Final Settlement relied on, that is an active allocation or Credit application on an obligation of a Lease whose Final Settlement is closed made at or before the closure, a Final Settlement Credit application, a record linked to a correction, or a Deposit receipt of a Deposit of such a Lease (use the `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction of that Settlement, owner answer 5 to Q-37); a Refund paid from the Payment's value that is not fully returned (Q-72 point 1); a Deposit it funded with an active forfeiture (staff first reverse it with the 6b draft, Q-72 point 2) or an active Deposit application; a pending `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft of the Payment, a pending `PAYMENT_MOVE` draft of one of its allocations, a pending Refund draft of the Payment or one of its Credits or Deposits, or a pending receipt reversal, forfeiture or Settlement draft of a Deposit it funded. Source digest: the Payment status and every listed record, amount, Refund and draft. Exact repeat replays; a reused draft id P5205.
      - Confirm `app.confirm_payment_return(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; human approver; READ COMMITTED; EXECUTE to the financial role): the draft lock; replay of an exact repeat; locks in the order the existing validators use: the Payment (`app.serialize_payment_state`, row FOR UPDATE), its Credits FOR UPDATE in id order, every allocation and Credit application target (`app.lock_payment_allocation_target`, id order), then every Deposit it funded (`app.serialize_deposit_state`, row FOR UPDATE, id order); the recorder's checks again and the digest (stale P5S02, the draft left pending); the CONFIRMED decision (result with the return id and the transaction id); the `payment_returns` row; then, in this order, each Credit application reversal (newest first), each Credit reversal, each allocation reversal (newest first), each Deposit receipt reversal and the Payment reversal, each dated with a strictly increasing instant (as `app.credit_excess_instant`), with the draft's reason, the approver as actor and the table's own authorization string; `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`; one audit event `payment.return.confirmed` with the draft id as correlation. The existing reconcile triggers refresh the Invoices and Charges; the debt comes back on its obligations and stays with their Customers; the Payment row and its Receipt never change. No confirm result or replay shows the decision's transaction id (the generic draft reads still do; 11c strips it).
      - The Settlement preview is not changed: a Settlement may close while a `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft is pending; when the closure relies on the Payment's records, the draft is then stale at its confirm (P5S02, it stays pending; as built, see (3) below) and staff withdraw it and record the `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction.
      - Service `PaymentReturnService` (`RecordPaymentReturnCommand`; `payments.reverse` records, `financial.approve` confirms, both checked before any connection; error mapping as the Payment move service; P5S02 stale; 55000 and P4904 conflicts). Routes in 11c.
      - 10e-1a as built (migration `20261120_116`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10e-1a-payment-return.md): as designed, with these differences. (1) A new preparer capability makes eight, so the two capability-scoped draft reads accept eight instead of seven (a relaxing exact-text patch; the read-only search found it), and two `test_lease_deposit_forfeiture.py` expectations changed (docs/010). (2) The Payment reversal row names `financial.approve` (its column has no fixed string); the receipt reversal is stamped with the statement time, as the 8r confirm; each Deposit is locked with its `lease-deposit-forfeiture:` advisory lock and then its row FOR UPDATE (as the 8r and Final Settlement confirms), not with `app.serialize_deposit_state`; the draft and the confirm list only the Credits that are not reversed (review round 3 notes). (3) Review round 1 fixes (docs/010 entry "after review round 1"): the relied-on rule also covers a Credit a Settlement or a correction used even when that use was later reversed or released; the recorder runs only at READ COMMITTED; the confirm locks only active records' targets, Charges before Invoices (after review round 2: in two passes as the Final Settlement confirm, every Charge in id order, then every Invoice in id order with the Charges' source Invoices); the moved-Credit pass is keyed on the Credit's own Payment; a draft recorded before a closure is stale at its confirm (P5S02), not refused with 55000; the owner's 8g case now applies part of the Credit to another Invoice of the Invoice Customer.
      - Tests (new module `test_payment_return_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`): the owner's case, a returned third-party Payment whose adjustment Credit was released to another Customer and partly applied to another Invoice of that Customer (8g block ended; the 8g one-call refusal still holds outside a return); a wrong-Unit moved Payment with a moved-value Credit of another Customer (slice 7 block ended); a Payment with a Reservation Security Deposit receipt; a Late-payment Charge allocation; the owner's condition: a Payment whose allocation paid an obligation of a closed Settlement before its closure is refused, and an allocation made after the closure is returned; refusals (a Refund not returned, an active forfeiture, pending drafts, Staff cannot confirm, a stale draft stays pending, replay, reused id); no transaction id in any result; ledger reconciliation; the Payment row unchanged; `payment_returns` append-only; least privilege; forward-only.
    - **10e-1b — `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction** (Q-72 points 2 and 3 answered (a), 2026-10-10; migration after 10e-1a): the 10e-1a writer core as an internal helper, run inside a `SETTLEMENT_CORRECTION` draft of variant `RETURNED_PAYMENT` (`final_settlements.prepare` records, `financial.approve` confirms) of the closed Settlement whose records the Payment funded: begin, the `payment_returns` row with `settlement_correction_id`, each reversal linked before it is written; in addition the Settlement's and its corrections' Credit applications of the Payment's Credits (they pass 8e-2 by the link) and every active Deposit application of a Deposit the Payment funded (10c-2a-1 records, newest first), then the receipt (it passes the closure check by the link). Locks in the 8e-3 order (the draft lock, the Settlement's draft lock, the Lease row FOR UPDATE, the lock function with the Payment and its Credits in its id-ordered pass, then the Deposits). No DL-032 step: the Credits are reversed whole and each Deposit then holds exactly its receipt, which is reversed, so no value is left to apply or refund; a Deposit that would still hold money is refused (55000). The debt that comes back stays on its Customers' ledgers (BR-SETTLE-016). Tests: the owner's cases (a bounced Payment whose Credit a Settlement Credit application used; one whose Security Deposit the Settlement applied); the Settlement row and snapshots unchanged; the 8e-2 refusal still holds outside the correction.
      - Owner answers of 2026-10-10 (fresh session; docs/010): Q-72 point 2 (a): a Deposit the Payment funded with an active forfeiture is handled in the same correction: the forfeiture is reversed as a linked record (DB-SETTLE-005 `deposit_disposition_reversal_id`, through the existing reversal function, as 10d), then the receipt, then the Payment; the Customer does not owe the Deposit amount (no new charge rule); a Deposit that would still hold money afterwards is refused (55000). Q-72 point 3 (a): a Payment whose records a closed Final Settlement other than the correction's own relied on is refused (55000), with a test; a known limit in docs/010.
      - Possible later work (owner answer to Q-72 point 3, option (b), not planned for V1-056): a multi-Settlement correction, one draft that writes one correction header per Settlement in one confirm, so one Payment used by two closed Final Settlements can be returned (a change to DB-SETTLE-003 and the 10a header rules; its own sub-slice). It can replace the point 3 refusal later without changing any record already written.
      - **10e-1b pre-code design** (2026-10-10, read only; head `20261120_116`, read from a throwaway database's catalog; migration `20261121_117`, so 10c-2b-5 takes the next free number): no owner question.
        - Recorder `app.record_settlement_returned_payment_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, input {payment_id, evidence_reference 1 to 500 characters}, reason 1 to 500, authorization)` (`final_settlements.prepare`; READ COMMITTED, as the 10e-1a recorder; EXECUTE to the financial role): an exact repeat replays, a reused id P5205; the 10e-1a `payment-return:` lock, so only one draft of a Payment waits, also against a `PAYMENT_RETURN` recorder; the Settlement of the Property (P0002) and closed (55000); the source is the 10e-1a source plus the Settlement and the active Deposit applications and active forfeitures of every Deposit the Payment funded; payload `variant` `RETURNED_PAYMENT`, `final_settlement_id`, `lease_id`, `payment_id`, the input, the source; the 16,000-byte draft limit (22023); the source digest as source revision.
        - Refusals (recorder and confirm; unknown Payment P0002, otherwise 55000): not RECEIVED; no record that this Settlement relied on (as built after review round 1, checked first: if another closed Settlement relied on them, use that Settlement's correction; if none did, use the `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft); a record that a closed Final Settlement other than this one also relied on (Q-72 point 3, a known limit); a Refund paid from the Payment's value that is not fully returned (Q-72 point 1, until 10e-1c); an active forfeiture of a funded Deposit whose governing Lease is not this Settlement's; an active Deposit application of another Settlement; a funded Deposit that would still hold money after the return (held value plus its active Deposit applications and forfeitures differs from the Payment's active receipts in it); a waiting draft that names the Payment or one of its records.
        - Confirm `app.confirm_settlement_returned_payment(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; a person; READ COMMITTED; replay; a draft whose sources changed is stale, P5S02, and stays pending). Lock order: the draft lock; the `payment-return:` lock of the Payment (as the 10e-1a recorder and confirm, before any row lock); then the 8e-3 order: the Settlement's draft lock, the Lease row FOR UPDATE, the lock function, then the funded Deposits' active forfeitures FOR UPDATE in id order. The lock function gets a `RETURNED_PAYMENT` branch (exact-text patch): no DL-032 plan; the Payment and its Credits that are not reversed join the id-ordered Payment and Credit passes; the targets of its active allocations and Credit applications and of the funded Deposits' active Deposit applications join the Charge and Invoice passes FOR UPDATE, also outside the Lease; the funded Deposits join the Deposit pass. Then the digest and the refusals again; the decision and the header (`app.begin_settlement_correction`); then the write steps.
        - Write steps (internal helper `app.post_settlement_returned_payment`, for 10e-1c to reuse; 10e-2 keeps the Payment, so it needs the helper split first (review round 1); the helper runs no refusal itself, so every caller runs the source and refusal check under the same locks first; the 10e-1a confirm is not changed): the `payment_returns` row with `settlement_correction_id` (relaxing exact-text patch of `app.validate_payment_return`: a `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft with no correction, or a `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction draft with its own correction); then, each linked before it is written, with the draft's reason, the approver as actor and a strictly increasing instant (except the forfeiture reversal, which keeps the reversal function's statement time, so it is dated before the header, as in 10d; review round 1): each Credit application reversal, newest first (the Settlement's and the corrections' pass the 8e-2 and remainder refusals by the link), each Credit reversal, each allocation reversal, newest first, each forfeiture reversal through the existing reversal function (as 10d: `deposits.correct`, the draft's evidence reference; Q-72 point 2), each Deposit application reversal, newest first (`financial.approve`, the 10c-2a-1 rules), each receipt reversal, and the Payment reversal; `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`; one `final_settlement.correction.confirmed` audit event. The receipt reversal is written by the helper itself, with the same command record and `deposit.receipt.reversed` audit event as the existing receipt reversal function, because that function stamps the statement time, which would date the receipt reversal before the Deposit application reversals and make the Deposit's history negative (refused). No DL-032 step: the Credits are reversed whole and each Deposit ends empty; no charge for the Deposit amount (Q-72 point 2).
        - Service: `RecordReturnedPaymentCorrectionCommand`, `SettlementCorrectionService.record_returned_payment` and `confirm_returned_payment` (stale P5S02). Routes in 11e.
        - Tests (new module `test_settlement_returned_payment_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`): the owner's cases (a bounced Payment whose Credit a Settlement Credit application used; one whose Security Deposit the Settlement applied) on a real closure; a kept Security Deposit whose Payment bounced (the forfeiture, the receipt and the Payment reversed, the Deposit empty, no charge); a Payment that two closed Settlements relied on is refused (Q-72 point 3); refusals (no record of this Settlement, Settlement not closed, unknown Payment, a Refund not returned, a waiting draft, a Deposit that would still hold money, Staff cannot confirm, stale draft stays pending, reused id, replay); the 8e-2 refusal still holds outside the correction; the Settlement row and closure snapshots unchanged; the `payment_returns` row check; ledger reconciliation; least privilege; forward-only. Unit tests for the command and the service.
    - **10e-1c — a Refund already paid from the returned value** (Q-72 point 1 answered (a), its detail confirmed 2026-10-10: with no closed Settlement the charge is an ordinary one-time Miscellaneous charge on the Lease the money came from; `EXTRA_CHARGE` only inside a correction): the owner's `EXTRA_CHARGE` in the same correction, with the record that balances the Refund's source, and the 10b helper split (the linked obligation, Invoice and issue writes of `app.confirm_settlement_extra_charge` move into an internal helper with no begin and no `ALL IMMEDIATE`; `source_reference` unique per charge; `app.settlement_extra_charge_result` returns arrays).
