      - **10e-1c pre-code design** (2026-10-11, read only; head `20261121_117`; migration `20261122_118` planned). Open: Q-2026-10-11-78 (who is charged and on which Lease; blocks the build).
        - What exists: (1) a Refund correction (`app.refund_corrections`, DB-REFUND-002) has the kinds `VALUE_CORRECTION` (a verified return) and `CORRECTION_REVERSAL`; its `source_balance_effect_usd_cents` gives value back to the Refund's source, and every reader of a source balance (`app.refund_net_value_at`, `app.payment_refunded_value_at`, `app.credit_refunded_value_at`, `app.deposit_has_unresolved_refund`, `app.deposit_balance_events`, the Customer Ledger's `REFUND_CORRECTED` event, which shows the kind) sums that effect, so a row of a new kind needs no reader change. (2) An Invoice with a Lease must belong to a signed Customer of that Lease (`app.validate_invoice_record`), and the one-time charge recorder (`app.record_one_time_charge`) bills a charge with a Lease only to the Primary Tenant; a Miscellaneous charge with no Lease may go to any Customer. (3) A third-party payer is not a signed Customer of the Lease. (4) A Refund paid from the returned value can be a Payment Refund (to the payer), a Credit Refund (the Credit's Customer, also a `CREDIT_CUSTOMER` Refund), or a Deposit Refund of a Deposit the Payment funded (a Settlement, correction or Pet Refund-review Refund, possibly paid to an agreed Signer; or a Reservation Deposit Refund, with no Lease).
        - Design: relaxing changes to `app.refund_corrections` (DB-REFUND-002 amendment, approved with option (a) of Q-72 point 1): the kind `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` (no verified-return fields, no reversed correction, effect above 0 and equal to the Refund's net value) and a new nullable unique column naming the linked charge (`recovery_obligation_id`, FK `authorized_nonrecurring_obligations`, required exactly for that kind, with the same amount); its checks accept the row only while the returned Payment's return is confirmed in the same transaction (`app.payment_return_in_transaction` of the Refund's source Payment, through its Credit or the Deposit's receipt), and for a Settlement or correction Refund only when it is linked to that correction (DB-SETTLE-005 `refund_correction_id`). The charge: with a closed Final Settlement of the charge's Lease, an `EXTRA_CHARGE` written through the split 10b helper and linked; otherwise an ordinary Miscellaneous one-time charge and its Invoice, issued in the same confirm. Both the `PAYMENT_RETURN` confirm (10e-1a) and the `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction (10e-1b) write, before their reversals, one recovery row and one charge per Refund that is not fully returned; their refusal of such a Refund is lifted only for the cases Q-78 allows. The sub-slice 10e-1b optional hardening (a refusal check inside the helper) is built first, because 10e-1c adds a caller path.
        - Tests: the owner's Q-72 example (a Security Deposit of 1,000 funded by one Payment, the Settlement applied 600 and refunded 400, then the Payment bounced: the 600 comes back as debt, the 400 Refund is recovered by a 400 charge to the Deposit owner, the receipt and the Payment are reversed, the Deposit ends at 0); a Credit Refund and a Payment Refund with no closed Settlement (ordinary charge); the refused cases Q-78 leaves; the 10b `EXTRA_CHARGE` tests unchanged after the helper split; ledger reconciliation; least privilege; forward-only.
        - After the owner answers of 2026-10-11 (Q-78: A, B, C; "the person who received the refund money pays it back"): the charge goes to the Refund's recipient for the exact net amount still out. Its Lease: a Deposit Refund, the Deposit's Lease; a Credit Refund, the Lease of the Invoice or Late-payment Charge whose release created the Credit, else the Lease the Payment names; a Payment Refund, the Lease the Payment names. The charge: an `EXTRA_CHARGE` (linked) when that Lease is the correction's own closed Settlement's and the recipient is its signed Customer; an ordinary Miscellaneous one-time charge on that Lease when the Lease has no closed Settlement and the recipient is its signed Customer (also a Signer who is not the Primary Tenant, owner answer C); otherwise (no Lease, or the recipient is not a signed Customer of it) a Miscellaneous one-time charge with no Lease (owner answer B). Choice for the owner (docs/010): when that Lease has a closed Final Settlement other than the correction's own, the return is refused (55000), as the Q-72 point 3 known limit, because a charge on that Lease is written only by a correction of its own Settlement. The `EXTRA_CHARGE` result of 10b keeps its shape (one charge), so its committed tests stay unchanged; the 10e-1c result lists its own charges.
        - Committed tests: lifting the refusal of a Refund that is not fully returned changes three committed assertions (10e-1a `test_each_refusal_of_the_source_state_is_checked` and `test_refusals_stale_drafts_withdrawal_and_reads`; 10e-1b `test_refusals_stale_drafts_and_a_deposit_that_would_still_hold_money`), so per the owner instruction of 2026-10-11 the whole of 10e-1c, and 10e-2 after it, waits for Q-2026-10-11-79. No 10e-1c code. Build notes kept for then: the 10b `EXTRA_CHARGE` writes move into a helper by replacing the block between two markers of the stored confirm, each found exactly once, so the 10b behaviour and tests stay unchanged; the recovery step runs right after the `payment_returns` row; the helper gains the explicit Property check of each receipt's Deposit.
        - The owner's check of 2026-10-11 on the correction's own receipt reversal is answered in the next report and in the 10e-1c record: the shared BEFORE INSERT check (`app.validate_deposit_receipt_reversal`) runs for both paths (state: active forfeiture, unresolved Refund, active Deposit application, waiting drafts, closure link); the amount is always the whole receipt; the function's own extra checks are the `deposits.correct` string, a non-blank reason and the Deposit's Property; the explicit Property check is only hardening (every receipt's Deposit has its Payment's Property by an existing check) and is added to the helper in 10e-1c, the next sub-slice that touches it.
        - Owner answers of 2026-10-11 (Q-2026-10-11-79 (a); the receipt reversal check accepted): 10e-1c is built. The three committed assertions change to the new result, each checking exactly who is charged, on which Lease or with no Lease, and the amount (names kept unless they would say the wrong thing; then renamed with their traceability entries in the same commit; the reviewer compares each with HEAD). The helper gains the explicit "Deposit belongs to this Property" check, and a test shows that the direct path (the correction's own receipt reversal) is refused in a bad state (moved forward from slice 12).
      - 10e-1c as built (migration `20261122_118`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10e-1c-payment-return-recovery.md; docs/010 and docs/004 DB-REFUND-002, DB-PAY-009, DB-DEP-007): as designed above, with the Q-78 charge cases: an `EXTRA_CHARGE` inside the correction (the correction's own closed Settlement's Lease, the person its signed Customer), an ordinary Miscellaneous one-time charge on an open Lease (the person its signed Customer, also a second Signer), else a Miscellaneous one-time charge with no Lease; each case tested. Refused (55000): a Reservation outcome Refund and a Refund of a Deposit two Payments funded (owner answers 1 and 2 to Q-2026-10-11-81: kept); a charge on a Lease whose closed Settlement the return cannot correct (until 10e-4 for a Payment that Settlement did not rely on). The three committed assertions changed under Q-79 (a) and the standing rule, names kept. Round 1 (independent 1 blocker, 4 should-fix; security 2 should-fix) fixed; round 2 had no blocker and no should-fix. Slice 12 items: in docs/010 (sub-slice 10e-1c entry) and the record.
      - Possible later work (owner answer 1 to Q-2026-10-11-81, not planned for V1-056): a cancelled Reservation's Deposit Refund paid from a bounced Payment, recovered through the Reservation outcome path with a no-Lease charge to the person refunded (owner answer B to Q-78); until then such a Payment cannot be returned in the system while that Refund is out (a known limit, tested).
    - **10e-4 — a Payment used on a Lease whose closed Final Settlement did not rely on it** (owner answer 3 to Q-2026-10-11-81, option (c); placed after 10e-3 and before 10c-2a-7, because it changes the same returned-Payment source, refusal and recovery code as 10e-1b and 10e-1c and reuses the 10e-1c charge; open: Q-2026-10-11-82, who is charged when the person refunded is not the Customer whose debt the Payment paid; nothing is built until the owner answers; while it waits, the next sub-slice goes ahead). The `RETURNED_PAYMENT` correction of a closed Final Settlement also takes a Payment that the Settlement did not rely on when the Payment's money was used on the Settlement's Lease after closure (the Payment names that Lease, or one of its allocations, Credit applications or Refund recoveries is on that Lease), a relaxing change to the correction's "did not rely on" refusal for this case only; the `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft keeps refusing such a Payment, with a message that names the correction. In the correction, the Payment's records are reversed as linked records (the debt comes back on its obligations and stays with their Customers, BR-SETTLE-016), and each Refund paid from its value whose charge goes on that Lease is recovered by an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on that Lease for the exact net amount (the 10e-1c helper; to the person refunded under recommendation (a) of Q-82, or to the Customer whose debt the Payment paid under (b)). A Payment whose records another closed Settlement relied on stays refused (the Q-72 point 3 known limit). Tests: the Q-81 example to the cent ($900 naming the closed Lease, $300 refunded, the transfer bounces: a $300 `EXTRA_CHARGE` on that Lease); a Payment with a post-closure allocation on that Lease (the debt comes back); the Q-82 two-Signer case under the owner's answer; a Payment used on another Lease still refused; the `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft still refused. The committed 10e-1c test `test_the_refused_cases_on_real_sources` changes its case (3) expectation under the standing rule (an owner answer ends that refusal), named in the report.
      - Owner answer to Q-2026-10-11-82 (2026-10-11): keep the design (the person who received a Refund paid from the bounced money pays it back), with one addition: when no one was refunded, the Customer whose debt the Payment paid is charged, the exact amount; a test for each case. Design: the reversed allocation puts the debt back on that Customer's obligation (no second charge, which would bill the same debt twice); the tests are the Q-82 two-Signer case (B refunded $300 is charged $300; A owes the $600 again) and the same Payment with nothing refunded (A owes the $600 again, no charge written).
      - **10e-4 pre-code design** (2026-10-13, read only; head `20261130_126` read from a throwaway database's catalog; migration `20261201_127`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-016, BR-PAY-042; owner answer (c) to Q-2026-10-11-81 point 3, answer to Q-2026-10-11-82; DB-PAY-009, DB-SETTLE-005). No owner question. What exists: the `RETURNED_PAYMENT` recorder and confirm already reverse every active record of the Payment (also outside the Lease), recover each Refund out by a charge (an `EXTRA_CHARGE` when its Lease is the correction's own closed Settlement's and the recipient is its signed Customer), and link each record; the only thing that stops the Q-81 case is the first refusal of `app.assert_settlement_returned_payment_allowed` (no record this Settlement relied on). The `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft refuses it through `app.assert_payment_return_recoveries_allowed` (a recovery on a closed Settlement's Lease).
        - Relaxing exact-text patches: `app.settlement_returned_payment_source` gains `used_on_lease` (true when the Payment names the Settlement's Lease, or an active allocation of the Payment, or an active application of one of its Credits that is not reversed, is on an Invoice of that Lease or a Late-payment Charge of such an Invoice); `app.assert_settlement_returned_payment_allowed` lets a Payment that no closed Settlement relied on through when `used_on_lease` is true or one of its planned recoveries is charged on this Settlement's Lease (`closed_final_settlement_id` is this Settlement); a missing `used_on_lease` counts as false (fail closed). The "another closed Settlement relied on it" refusals stay (the Q-72 point 3 known limit). Message only: for the `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft (no correction), the refusal of a recovery on a closed Settlement's Lease now names the correction ("...; return the Payment through a returned-Payment correction of that Final Settlement"); a correction of another Settlement keeps the old message.
        - Not changed: the recorder, the confirm, the write helper, the lock function (it already locks every target, also outside the Lease), the recovery writer, the service. The `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft still returns a Payment whose only use on a closed Lease after closure is an allocation or Credit application with nothing refunded (no closed Settlement relied on it and no recovery is charged there); the correction can return the same Payment too, with the records linked. Note for the owner, not blocking: closing the `PAYMENT_RETURN` path for such a Payment would be a tightening (10h decides the one-call changes after closure).
        - Tests (new module `test_returned_payment_after_closure_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`), each on a real closure, to the cent: the Q-81 example (a 900 Payment naming the closed Lease, 300 refunded to the Customer, the transfer bounces: a 300 `EXTRA_CHARGE` on that Lease, the Payment reversed, the Settlement row unchanged); the Q-82 two-Signer case (B's 900 Payment naming the Lease pays 600 of A's `EXTRA_CHARGE` Invoice and 300 is refunded to B: B is charged 300 by an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on the Lease, A owes the 600 again); the same Payment with nothing refunded (A owes the 600 again, no charge written); a Payment used on another Lease is still refused; the `PAYMENT_RETURN` draft still refuses the Q-81 Payment with the new message; the refusal function on hand-built sources (each way in, a missing key, another Settlement); ledger reconciliation; least privilege and forward-only of migration 127. Committed tests changed under the standing rule (owner answer (c) to Q-81 ends the refusal): `test_the_refused_cases_on_real_sources` case (3) (the correction is now recorded; the `PAYMENT_RETURN` refusal has the new message), and the two `PAYMENT_RETURN` message assertions of `test_the_cases_a_return_still_refuses`; the 27 head-revision assertions move to `20261201_127`.
      - 10e-4 as built (migration `20261201_127`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10e-4-returned-payment-after-closure.md; docs/004 DB-PAY-009 section, docs/010): as designed above, with the review changes: in the relaxed branch a source without a `relied_on` key is refused (55000, "The returned-Payment correction source is incomplete"), a `relied_on` that is not a list cannot be read (22023), and only a JSON `true` `used_on_lease` counts (review round 1, security). Eight tests in the new module (the owner's Q-81 and Q-82 examples in dollars as cents, nothing refunded, the allocation branch with a reversed allocation as a control, the Credit-application branch, another Lease refused, the check on built sources, least privilege and forward-only). The recovery charge keeps the 10e-1c rules, so a Payment that names no Lease and is used on the closed Lease gets a no-Lease charge for its Refund (Q-2026-10-13-98 asks the owner to confirm; nothing waits). Rounds 1, 2 and 3. Next in the order: 10e-3c, 10e-3d, 10c-2a-7, 10c-3c-1, 10c-3c-2, 10f, 10g, 10e-2d, 10e-2b-3, 10e-2b-4, then 10h.
    - Not changed: the one-call `app.reverse_payment_command`; the one-call allocation and Credit application reversals (10h); the 8r receipt reversal draft.
- **10e-2 — `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION`** (owner answer to Q-2026-10-08-56; right after 10e; must be built before V1-056 ends): a Lease Deposit receipt that is wrong while its Payment is real (meant for Rent, the wrong Deposit, the wrong amount), after closure. A variant of the `SETTLEMENT_CORRECTION` draft kind (`final_settlements.prepare` records with a reason and evidence, `financial.approve` confirms), following every Settlement rule and the DL-032 order: as linked records, the Settlement's Deposit applications of that Deposit are reversed (10c-2a-1), then the receipt; a Settlement Refund already paid from that Deposit becomes an `EXTRA_CHARGE` to the person who received it (the 10e rule as the Q-2026-10-11-78 answer set it; text updated 2026-10-12 under owner answer (a) to Q-2026-10-12-94; it said "the Deposit owner"); the freed Payment value becomes the payer's Customer Credit, which first pays that Customer's open debt on the Lease through the shared step, and only the rest is refunded or kept as Credit. Until it exists, the 10c-2b-2 refusal leaves such a receipt with no fix after closure (docs/010 known gap).
  - **10e-2 pre-code design** (2026-10-11, read only; head `20261122_118`; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-008, BR-SETTLE-016, DL-032, BR-CREDIT-002, BR-CREDIT-003, BR-CREDIT-004, BR-AUTH-010; DB-DEP-007, DB-SETTLE-005, DB-CREDIT-001; owner answer (a) to Q-2026-10-08-56, answer 5 to Q-2026-10-08-55, the Q-2026-10-11-78 rule; migration: the next free number when built). Open: Q-2026-10-11-83 (three points; blocks the build). No code.
    - What exists: (1) a Deposit receipt (DB-DEP-002) is written only by `app.create_reservation`, so every Deposit has exactly one receipt, of one Payment; no function writes a new receipt into another Deposit or for another amount, and a receipt reversal is always the whole receipt (`deposit_receipt_reversals.deposit_receipt_id` unique). (2) After closure a receipt of the Lease's Deposit is reversed only through a linked correction (P5S01, sub-slice 10c-2b-2); the shared BEFORE INSERT check also refuses it while the Deposit has an active forfeiture, a Refund not fully returned, an active Deposit application or a waiting draft (55000). (3) The `RETURNED_PAYMENT` write helper (`app.post_settlement_returned_payment`) reverses, linked, the Payment's Credit applications, Credits and allocations, the funded Deposits' forfeitures (through the 6b reversal function), Deposit applications and receipts (the shared writer `app.write_payment_return_receipt_reversal`), then the Payment; 10e-2 needs only the Deposit part, for one receipt, and keeps the Payment and its other records. (4) The 10e-1c writer `app.write_payment_return_recoveries` recovers a Refund paid from a Deposit by a `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row and a charge to the person who received the money (an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on the Settlement's own Lease); its check `app.refund_recovery_allowed` accepts a row only inside a confirmed `payment_returns` row of the Refund's Payment, so 10e-2 (no return) needs a second accepted context. (5) The shared DL-032 step (`app.settlement_correction_plan_result`, `app.post_settlement_correction_plan`) applies existing Credits (step 2, Credit before Deposits after closure, owner answer to Q-2026-10-07-42) and held Deposit money, then Deposit Refunds; a Credit a correction creates is named in the plan through the draft's `plan_overlay` and `app.settlement_correction_credit_id` (today only a Credit a correction's own credit Adjustment releases, BR-CREDIT-014). (6) DB-SETTLE-005 has no link for a Credit a correction recognizes; a released Credit is found through its release row. (7) BR-CREDIT-002: unallocated Payment value becomes Customer Credit only after the payer authorizes it, never automatically; the only exception is BR-CREDIT-014 (a credit Adjustment's release). BR-CREDIT-004: Credit is never silently converted into a Deposit.
    - Design (subject to Q-83): a new variant `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` of the `SETTLEMENT_CORRECTION` draft kind. Recorder `app.record_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, input {deposit_receipt_id, evidence_reference 1..500, plan}, reason)` under `final_settlements.prepare` (READ COMMITTED; one waiting draft per receipt; the payload stores the source: the receipt, its Payment and payer, the Deposit, its active Deposit applications of this Settlement and of its corrections, its Refunds not fully returned with their planned recoveries, and the overlay naming the freed value); confirm `app.confirm_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction` (`financial.approve`, a person, READ COMMITTED, replay, stale P5S02 with the draft left pending; the 8e-3 lock order plus the receipt's Payment and the Lease key locks of 10e-1c). In one transaction, each record linked before it is written: the decision and the header; the Deposit's application reversals, newest first (the debt comes back on its obligations and stays with their Customers, BR-SETTLE-016); one recovery per Refund paid from the Deposit that is not fully returned (the 10e-1c writer: the person who received it is charged the exact net amount, an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on this Lease when that person is its signed Customer, else a no-Lease charge); the receipt reversal (the shared writer); the payer's Customer Credit of the freed amount (Q-83 point 1); then the shared DL-032 step with the Credit first, its rest refunded (the step's Refund forms) or kept as Credit. Refused (55000): a receipt not of this Settlement's Lease; a Deposit with an active forfeiture (Q-83 point 3); a Deposit application of another Final Settlement; a Reservation outcome Refund or a second receipt (as 10e-1c); a waiting draft that names the receipt, the Deposit or its Payment. Relaxing changes: `app.refund_recovery_allowed` also accepts a recovery linked to a confirmed `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` of the Refund's Deposit; DB-SETTLE-005 gains the link `customer_credit_id` (a Credit a correction recognizes; deferrable, unique, in the one-link check); `app.settlement_correction_credit_id` maps the overlay key of the freed receipt to that Credit. Helper split: the Deposit-application reversal loop of `app.post_settlement_returned_payment` moves into an internal helper by an exact span replacement (the 10e-1a and 10e-1b tests unchanged; a committed test that must change stops the work for the owner).
    - Tests: the owner's three examples to the cent, each after a real closure: (1) a receipt meant for Rent (Security Deposit 1,000 from one Payment; the Settlement applied 600 and refunded 400; the correction reverses the 600, recovers the 400 by a 400 `EXTRA_CHARGE`, reverses the receipt, recognizes 1,000 Credit, which pays the 600 and the 400 charge); (2) the wrong Deposit and (3) the wrong amount as Q-83 point 2 decides; a payer who is not a signed Customer (the Credit pays nothing of the Lease; refunded or kept); a Deposit Refund to an agreed Signer recovered from that Signer; the refusals; the 8e-1 and 8e-2 refusals passed only by the link; ledger reconciliation; least privilege; forward-only; the 10b, 10e-1a and 10e-1b tests unchanged after the helper split.
    - Owner answers to Q-2026-10-11-83 (2026-10-11): point 1, BR-CREDIT-002 is not amended (the freed value becomes Credit only with the payer's authorization; until then it stays unallocated with the Payment); point 2, a new function writes the right receipt (the right Deposit or the right amount, no Credit step; new functions only, no tightening); point 3 (a), a kept Deposit is refused until a `FORFEITURE_REVERSAL` runs. Pre-code check of these answers (2026-10-11, read only, head `20261122_118`; STOPPED before code, Q-2026-10-11-86): point 2 needs more than new functions: `UNIQUE (deposit_id, payment_id)` refuses a second receipt from the same Payment in the same Deposit (a reversed receipt still counts); the receipt check requires a Security Deposit's first receipt to equal its accepted amount and all receipts to stay within the required amount (so a wrong agreed amount also needs a required-amount decrease); no function writes a Pet Deposit receipt today. Recommendation (a): build 10e-2 without the new receipt (applications reversed, Refunds charged back, the whole receipt reversed, the freed value left with the Payment), the right receipt as a later sub-slice 10e-2b after the owner's word on the three receipt-rule points.
    - Owner answer to Q-2026-10-11-86 (2026-10-12): (a). 10e-2 is built now without the new receipt; the freed money stays with the Payment; the right receipt is sub-slice 10e-2b (below), which waits for Q-2026-10-12-87 (the three receipt rules); the gap must close before V1-056 ends.
    - **10e-2 pre-code design under the answers** (2026-10-12, read only, head `20261123_119` read from a throwaway database's catalog; migration `20261124_120`; replaces the 10e-2 entry's first line and the "Design (subject to Q-83)" above where they differ, for example the freed value stays with the Payment instead of becoming Credit; no owner question):
      - Variant `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` of the `SETTLEMENT_CORRECTION` draft kind. Recorder `app.record_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, input {deposit_receipt_id, evidence_reference 1..500}, reason 1..500, authorization)` (`final_settlements.prepare`; READ COMMITTED; EXECUTE to the financial role): replay of an exact repeat, a reused id P5205; the 10e-1a `payment-return:` lock of the receipt's Payment, so a receipt correction, a `PAYMENT_RETURN` and a `RETURNED_PAYMENT` draft of that Payment never wait together; the Settlement of the Property (P0002) and closed (55000); payload `variant`, `final_settlement_id`, `lease_id`, `deposit_receipt_id`, `deposit_id`, `payment_id`, the input and the source; the 16,000-byte draft limit; the source digest as source revision.
      - Source (`app.settlement_deposit_receipt_correction_source`): the receipt (Deposit, Payment, amount, reversed or not), the Payment (status, payer), the Deposit (type, Lease, held value, active forfeiture, other active receipts), its active Deposit applications (with their Final Settlement), its recoveries (the 10e-1c `app.payment_return_recoveries` entries of this Deposit only: each Refund paid from it that is not fully returned, the person who received it, the Lease and charge form), and the waiting drafts that name the receipt, the Deposit, its Payment or a recovered Refund.
      - Refusals (recorder, confirm and the write helper; unknown receipt or a receipt of another Property P0002, otherwise 55000): the receipt already reversed; its Deposit not of this Settlement's Lease; the Payment not RECEIVED (a guard); an active forfeiture (Q-83 point 3 (a): reverse it with a `FORFEITURE_REVERSAL` first); a Deposit application of another Final Settlement (a guard); the 10e-1c recovery refusals (a Reservation outcome Refund, a Deposit two Payments funded, a charge on a Lease whose closed Settlement this correction cannot correct); a Refund of the Deposit not fully returned with no planned recovery; a Deposit that would still hold money afterwards (held value plus its active applications plus its recovered Refunds differs from the receipt); a waiting draft.
      - Confirm `app.confirm_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; a person; READ COMMITTED; replay; a draft whose sources changed is stale, P5S02, and stays pending). Locks: the draft lock; the `payment-return:` lock of the receipt's Payment; the Settlement's draft lock; the `invoice-lease:` locks of the Settlement's Lease and of every recovery charge's Lease in one id-ordered pass; the Lease row FOR UPDATE; the correction lock function with a new `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` branch (no DL-032 plan; the receipt's Payment joins the Payment pass; the targets of the Deposit's active applications join the Charge and Invoice passes FOR UPDATE; the Deposit is in the Lease's Deposit pass). Then the digest, the refusals, the decision and the header (`app.begin_settlement_correction`), and the write helper `app.post_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction`, which runs the source, digest and refusal check itself (as the hardened 10e-1b helper), then writes, each record linked before it is written, with the draft's reason and the approver: each recovery (the 10e-1c writer `app.write_payment_return_recoveries`: a `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row and a charge of the exact net amount to the person who received the money, an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on this Lease when that person is its signed Customer, else a no-Lease charge); the Deposit's application reversals, newest first (the debt comes back on its obligations and stays with their Customers, BR-SETTLE-016); the receipt reversal (the shared writer `app.write_payment_return_receipt_reversal`). Then `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`, one `final_settlement.correction.confirmed` audit event and the result (the correction, the receipt and its reversal, the application reversals, the recoveries, the Payment's available value). No transaction id in any result.
      - No Payment reversal, no Credit, no DL-032 step (as `RETURNED_PAYMENT`): the Deposit ends empty and the freed amount stays unallocated with the Payment (Q-83 point 1: BR-CREDIT-002 not amended). The debt that came back stays open until staff allocate the Payment's freed value (allocations to a closed Settlement's obligations stay open, owner answer 7 to Q-2026-10-05-20) or the payer authorizes Credit (`app.recognize_customer_credit`), which the next correction's DL-032 step uses first (owner answer to Q-2026-10-07-42).
      - Relaxing exact-text patches: `app.refund_recovery_allowed` also accepts a recovery inside a `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION` confirmed by the same approver in this transaction whose receipt (not yet reversed) is in the Refund's Deposit (the other conditions unchanged: the charge, its amount and Customer, and every closed Settlement the Refund belongs to links the row); `app.write_payment_return_recoveries` words the charge for this variant ("paid from a corrected Deposit receipt"; the 10e-1a and 10e-1b texts unchanged); `app.lock_settlement_correction_plan` gains the branch above. Helper split (the design above): the Deposit-application reversal loop of `app.post_settlement_returned_payment` moves by an exact span replacement into the internal `app.write_correction_deposit_application_reversals(correction, approver, reason, deposits, at)`, which both helpers call (the 10e-1b behaviour and tests unchanged).
      - Service: `RecordDepositReceiptCorrectionCommand`, `SettlementCorrectionService.record_deposit_receipt_correction` and `confirm_deposit_receipt_correction` (stale P5S02). Routes in 11e.
      - Tests (new module `test_settlement_deposit_receipt_correction_migration.py`, last in `scripts/database-test.sh`), each after a real closure: the Pet Deposit receipt of the 8c normal-end Settlement (20,000: 12,500 and 2,100 of debt come back on their lines, the 5,400 Settlement Refund is recovered by a 5,400 `EXTRA_CHARGE` to the Deposit owner, the receipt is reversed, the Pet Deposit ends at 0, the Payment stays RECEIVED with 20,000 more available); the Security Deposit receipt (10,000 back as debt, no recovery) and the freed value then allocated to the debt that came back; a kept Deposit refused, then accepted after a `FORFEITURE_REVERSAL` correction (whose Refund is recovered); a kept Deposit refunded to the agreed second Signer and then corrected (that Signer is charged); the refusals; the 8e-1 receipt-reversal refusal still holds outside the correction; a recovery row outside a correction still refused; the Settlement row and snapshots unchanged; ledger reconciliation; least privilege; forward-only. Unit tests for the command and the service. The 10e-1a, 10e-1b and 10e-1c tests stay unchanged; the 27 head-revision assertions move to `20261124_120`.
    - 10e-2 as built (migration `20261124_120`; record ops/reviews/V1-056-slice-10e-2-deposit-receipt-correction.md; docs/004 DB-DEP-007 and DB-REFUND-002, docs/010): as designed above; the ends-empty refusal says "would not end empty"; no committed test changed except the 27 head-revision assertions. Rounds 1 and 2 (round 2 no blocker, no should-fix); slice 11e and 12 items in docs/010.
  - **10e-2b — the right receipt** (owner answers to Q-2026-10-11-83 point 2 and Q-2026-10-11-86 (a); Q-2026-10-12-87 answered 2026-10-12, see the sub-bullet below; built right after 10c-2b-6 and before 10e-3 (owner order of 2026-10-12; first placed after 10g)): inside a `DEPOSIT_RECEIPT_CORRECTION`, a new linked receipt of the same Payment's freed value into the right Deposit or for the right amount, after the wrong receipt's reversal; the draft's DL-032 plan names the new receipt's money, and the correction's step applies it in the DL-032 order and refunds the rest. Built after 10c-2b-6 under the answers below.
    - Owner answers to Q-2026-10-12-87 (2026-10-12): rule 1 (a), at most one active receipt per Payment in each Deposit (the key is replaced by that check; a reversed receipt stays in the history and no longer blocks a new one); rule 2 (a), inside a correction only a receipt may differ from the accepted amount, never above the required amount, the correction's reason explains it, the required amount does not change; rule 3 (a), a Pet Deposit receipt writer inside a correction only, refused while the Pet Deposit is kept. Relaxing or new-function changes only; a tightening stops the work for the owner. Placement (owner): right after 10c-2b-6, before 10e-3. Its pre-code design is written when 10c-2b-6 is committed.
    - **10e-2b pre-code design** (2026-10-12, read only, head `20261125_121`; functions read from a throwaway database's catalog at `20261124_120`, which the 10c-2b-6 patch does not touch; BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-008, BR-SETTLE-016, DL-032, BR-AUTH-010; DB-DEP-002, DB-SETTLE-005; owner answers (a), (a), (a) to Q-2026-10-12-87; migration `20261126_122` planned). Open: Q-2026-10-12-88 (a new receipt into a Deposit whose termination ground forfeits it). No code.
      - What exists: (1) `UNIQUE (deposit_id, payment_id)` is the only rule that counts a reversed receipt; nothing relies on it (no `ON CONFLICT`; the only receipt insert outside tests is `app.create_reservation`, into a new Deposit; no test names the key). (2) `app.validate_deposit_receipt` (BEFORE INSERT; locks the Deposit FOR UPDATE): a Security Deposit's first active receipt equals the accepted amount and all active receipts stay within the required amount; a Pet Deposit's stay within its required (approved) amount; `app.validate_payment_deposit_source`: a RECEIVED Payment, its payer the Deposit's Customer (or the Lease for a third-party Payment), within the Payment's available value. (3) The shared DL-032 step `app.post_settlement_correction_plan` runs `app.settlement_correction_plan_result` again at the confirm on the real state and requires the result the draft recorded with its overlay (deposit ids, open total, closing balances) to be equal; the overlay names only existing obligations (absolute amounts), adds to a Deposit's held value (never below 0) and adds Credits. (4) The sub-slice 10e-1c recovery charge is written with random ids (`app.write_correction_one_time_charge`), so a draft cannot name it; when its recipient is the Lease's signed Customer it is open debt of this Lease at the confirm-time step, so the record-time result would differ and a Refund of the rest would be refused while it stays open. (5) A Deposit with a forfeiture ground is `NOTHING_HELD` while empty and never forfeited, `FORFEITURE_REQUIRED` once it holds money, `KEPT` while forfeited, `DL_032` after its forfeiture is reversed (Q-88).
      - Input: the 10e-2 input gains optional `receipts` (1 or 2 items `{deposit_id, amount_usd_cents}`, distinct Deposits of the Settlement's Lease, each above 0, together at most the wrong receipt's amount: the Payment never gives more than the freed value) and `plan` (`{steps, pet_remainder}`, the shared DL-032 plan); both or neither (neither is 10e-2 as built, its tests unchanged).
      - Refusals (55000 unless named; record, confirm and the write helper): a new receipt's Deposit not of this Lease; a kept Deposit (owner rule 3 for a Pet Deposit; the same for a Security Deposit, as Q-83 point 3); a Deposit with a forfeiture ground until Q-88 is answered; a Security receipt above the required amount and a receipt the Payment cannot fund (the existing checks, 23514); a plan that does not apply or refund all the new money (the shared check).
      - Confirm writes, each linked before it is written, after the 10e-2 writes (recoveries, application reversals, the wrong receipt's reversal): the new receipts (new internal writer `app.write_correction_deposit_receipt(correction, approver, deposit, payment, amount, at)`, no grant, the only Pet Deposit receipt writer, linked by the new DB-SETTLE-005 link `deposit_receipt_id`), then the shared DL-032 step (Credit first, the Pet Deposit on pet damage, the Security Deposit, the Pet Deposit remainder, Refunds of the rest). The money the receipts do not take stays unallocated with the Payment (Q-83 point 1).
      - Recorder: as 10e-2, plus the overlay: each obligation whose Deposit applications are reversed, at its open amount plus those applications; each planned recovery charge on this Lease as a new obligation of its recipient, under a placeholder line id (`md5('deposit-receipt-recovery:' || draft || ':' || refund)`); each named Deposit's held value after the correction; the draft stores `plan`, `plan_result` and `plan_overlay` as the other plan variants.
      - Relaxing patches: the key `UNIQUE (deposit_id, payment_id)` is dropped and `app.validate_deposit_receipt` refuses a second active receipt of one Payment in one Deposit (23505, as the key; rule 1); in the same check a Security Deposit's first active receipt may differ from the accepted amount when a correction of the Lease's closed Final Settlement, confirmed in this transaction, links it (rule 2; never above the required amount, which does not change); DB-SETTLE-005 gains `deposit_receipt_id` (deferrable, unique, in the one-link check; `app.link_settlement_correction_effect` and `app.settlement_correction_linked` accept it); `app.settlement_correction_plan_result` accepts two optional overlay keys, `new_obligations` (placeholder keys with their Customer and amount, never pet damage) and `deposits_after` (absolute held values); `app.post_settlement_correction_plan` maps each placeholder target to the real recovery charge line (the correction's linked `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row of that Refund, its obligation, its Invoice line) before the confirm-time check and the writes; `app.lock_settlement_correction_plan`'s 10e-2 branch also locks the new receipts' Deposits and the plan's targets.
      - Service: `RecordDepositReceiptCorrectionCommand` gains the optional receipts and plan (both or neither). Routes in 11e.

      - **10e-2c pre-code design** (2026-10-12, read only, head `20261127_123` read from a throwaway database's catalog; owner answers to Q-2026-10-12-88: point 1 (b), point 2 (c); BR-SETTLE-013, BR-SETTLE-008, DL-032, BR-DEP-019, BR-DEP-022, BR-DEP-023, BR-DEP-026, BR-AUTH-010; DB-SETTLE-005, DB-DEP-002; migration `20261128_124` planned):
        - What exists: (1) `app.lease_deposit_settlement_path` gives a Deposit's ground, forfeiture (`NONE`, `ACTIVE`, `REVERSED`) and path; 10e-2b-1 refuses a right receipt into a Deposit with a ground (55000, "not supported yet"). (2) The forfeiture insert check (`app.validate_lease_deposit_disposition`) refuses a forfeiture of a closed Settlement's Deposit unless a correction confirmed in this transaction links it (`app.settlement_correction_linked(..., 'deposit_dispositions', ...)`), but DB-SETTLE-005 has no forfeiture link yet. (3) The only Lease forfeiture writers (`app.record_lease_deposit_disposition_with_context` through the slice 6 draft, and the owner one-call) date the row at `statement_timestamp()`, and `app.lease_deposit_forfeiture_source` reads the held value at `statement_timestamp()`; inside a correction the new receipt is dated later (after every earlier record of the confirm), so both would see no money and the Deposit history check (`app.validate_deposit_history_budget`) would see a negative balance. (4) The context check (`app.require_lease_deposit_disposition_context`, run by the BEFORE INSERT trigger on every Lease forfeiture) re-checks the termination record, the ground's rule and the Deposit state version; the 10c-2b-6 check refuses a forfeiture of pay-back money.
        - Input: each right receipt item may carry `outcome` (`DL_032` or `FORFEIT`) with `outcome_reason` (1..500 characters), both or neither. Service: `CorrectionDepositReceipt` gains them (optional, both or neither).
        - Rules (recorder, confirm and the write helper; 55000): point 1, a Deposit with a ground that was never forfeited (`NONE`): the same correction forfeits the new receipt's money on the termination's ground; an `outcome` there is refused (staff choose nothing). Point 2, a Deposit with a ground whose forfeiture was reversed (`REVERSED`): an `outcome` is required; `DL_032` keeps the 10e-2b path (the plan applies the money and refunds the rest), `FORFEIT` forfeits the new money again on the same ground, its reason the `outcome_reason`. A Deposit without a ground takes no `outcome`. A kept Deposit stays refused (Q-83 point 3). Only the new receipt's money is forfeited: refused when the Deposit would hold other money after the correction or has a pay-back count (a Pet Deposit with returned Pet Refund-review money; Q-2026-10-12-93, a refusal until the owner answers).
        - Recorder: the targets carry `outcome`, `outcome_reason` and the pay-back count; a new internal `app.settlement_deposit_receipt_forfeitures(source, targets, reason)` lists each forfeiture (Deposit, type, amount, ground rule, reason, basis `TERMINATION_GROUND` or `STAFF_CHOICE`); the draft stores it as `deposit_receipt_forfeitures` (only when not empty) so the approver sees each forfeiture; the overlay's `deposits_after` leaves forfeited money out, so the plan neither applies nor refunds it.
        - Confirm (write helper): the forfeitures are computed again under the locks and must equal the draft's (P5S02, the draft stays pending); after the right receipts and before the shared DL-032 step, each forfeiture is written by a new internal writer `app.write_correction_deposit_forfeiture(property, correction, approver, deposit, amount, reason, at)` (no grant): only a forfeiture its correction's draft names, only inside that correction's confirm (as the receipt writer); the ground rule and the termination event read again; the Deposit holds exactly the amount beyond its pay-back value; linked first (new DB-SETTLE-005 link `deposit_disposition_id`, deferrable, unique, in the one-link check; the link functions rebuilt as in 10e-2b-1), then its disposition context and the disposition row (authorization reference `deposits.dispose` as the table requires, the termination date as effective date, dated after the receipt), the Deposit audit event (`financial.approve`, correlated with the correction) and the Deposit command record; every existing insert check runs (context, ground rule, state version, closed-Settlement link, held value, pay-back limit, history). The result gains `deposit_disposition_ids`.

(2) The general Refund correction check (`app.validate_general_refund_correction`) refuses every row on a Settlement or correction Refund (23514) or a Pet Refund-review Refund after closure (P5S01) unless `app.settlement_refund_return_allowed` (a `REFUND_RETURN` return) or `app.refund_recovery_allowed` (a 10e-1c or 10e-2 recovery) passes it; its `CORRECTION_REVERSAL` branch reads the available value and the Payment status only through `pet_refund_review_id`, so for a Settlement or correction Deposit Refund both are NULL and an over-reversal would not be refused (the 10c-2b-2a note). (3) Every reader of a Refund's net value, a Deposit's held value, a Credit's available value and the Pet Deposit pay-back count (`app.lease_deposit_payback_count`) already sums a `CORRECTION_REVERSAL` row. (4) The left value of an earlier return-only correction (`app.settlement_held_deposit_value`, `app.settlement_held_credit_value`) sums its returns and does not look at reversals. (5) The 10e-1c recovery writer `app.write_payment_return_recoveries` takes a list of Refunds and writes, for each, a charge to the person who received the money (an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on the correction's Lease when that person is its signed Customer, else a no-Lease charge) and a `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row; `app.write_correction_deposit_application_reversals` reverses every active application of the Deposits it is given (too wide here).
      - Variant `RETURN_REVERSAL` of the `SETTLEMENT_CORRECTION` draft kind. Recorder `app.record_settlement_return_reversal_draft(property, actor, draft, settlement, input {settlement_correction_id, evidence_reference 1..500}, reason 1..500, authorization)` (`final_settlements.prepare`; READ COMMITTED; EXECUTE to the financial role): an exact repeat replays, a reused id P5205; a `return-reversal:` lock of the earlier correction, so only one draft of it waits; the Settlement of the Property (P0002) and closed (55000); payload `variant`, `final_settlement_id`, `lease_id`, `held_from_settlement_correction_id` (the earlier correction), the returned Refund, its source (Credit or Deposit), the input and the source; the 16,000-byte draft limit; the source digest as source revision.
      - Which records are undone (the owner's "paid from the returned value"): the uses of the returned Refund's own source (its Credit C, or its Deposit D) by the earlier correction and by every confirmed `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` that names it: their active Credit applications from C, their active Deposit applications from D, and their Refunds from C or D that still have a net value. Records they wrote from any other Credit or Deposit are real money paid to real debt and stay.
      - Source (`app.settlement_return_reversal_source_state(settlement, earlier)`): the earlier correction (its draft, its return row and amount, the returned Refund, C or D), whether its return is reversed, the linked held-value corrections, the records to undo (ids, targets, amounts), the recoveries (each Refund, its recipient, its net amount, its Lease or no Lease, whether the recipient is the Deposit's own Customer), the later returns of those Refunds, the source's value (C's available value or D's held value) and latest event.
      - Refusals (recorder and confirm; an unknown earlier correction, or one of another Settlement or of another variant, P0002; otherwise 55000): its return already reversed; a Refund to recover that has its own return recorded (reverse that later return first); a Refund to recover paid to someone other than the Deposit's own Customer (Q-2026-10-12-94, until answered); the source would not hold the returned amount after the undo ("The returned value is no longer available; undo its other use first": C's available value or D's held value, plus the undone applications and recoveries, is below the returned amount, because the money was used by a path this correction does not undo); nothing changed since the record (digest, P5S02 at the confirm, the draft stays pending). A waiting `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` naming the earlier correction becomes stale at its own confirm (its left value changes).
      - Confirm `app.confirm_settlement_return_reversal(property, approver, draft, authorization)` (`financial.approve`; a person; READ COMMITTED; replay; stale P5S02, the draft stays pending). Locks: the draft lock; the `return-reversal:` lock; the Settlement's draft lock; the `invoice-lease:` lock of the Lease (the recovery charges); the Lease row FOR UPDATE; the correction lock function with a new `RETURN_REVERSAL` branch (no DL-032 plan; C joins the Credit pass; the targets of the applications to undo join the Charge and Invoice passes FOR UPDATE; D is a Deposit of the Lease, in the Deposit pass). Then the digest and the refusals, the decision and the header (`app.begin_settlement_correction`), then, each record linked before it is written, with the draft's reason and the approver as actor, each dated after the last: the Credit application reversals, newest first (`credits.applications.reverse`, as 10e-1b); the Deposit application reversals, newest first (`financial.approve`, through a new internal writer of named applications, as 10c-2a-1); the recoveries (the 10e-1c writer: a charge of the exact net amount to the person who received the money, then a `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row); then the `CORRECTION_REVERSAL` row of the earlier return (`refunds.corrections.reverse`, the approver, the draft's reason and evidence, effect minus the returned amount). `SET CONSTRAINTS ALL IMMEDIATE`; one `final_settlement.correction.confirmed` audit event; the result (the correction, the reversed return, the reversals, the recoveries). No DL-032 step and no transaction id in any result: the debt that comes back stays on its obligations and with their Customers (BR-SETTLE-016), and the recovery charges are new debt.
      - Relaxing exact-text patches: `app.validate_general_refund_correction` accepts the reversal row when the new internal `app.settlement_return_reversal_allowed(row)` is true (a `CORRECTION_REVERSAL` under `refunds.corrections.reverse` of exactly the earlier correction's return, whole amount, the draft's reason and evidence, by the approver of a `RETURN_REVERSAL` correction of the Refund's own Settlement confirmed in this transaction that links it); its reversal branch reads D's held value and skips the Payment status for every Deposit Refund (`source_deposit_id`, the 10c-2b-2a note), so the source check runs for every Refund; `app.refund_recovery_allowed` also accepts a recovery linked to a confirmed `RETURN_REVERSAL` correction (same approver, this transaction) of a Refund that the earlier correction or a linked `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` wrote from the returned Refund's source; `app.write_payment_return_recoveries` words the charge for this variant ("paid from a Refund return recorded by mistake"); `app.lock_settlement_correction_plan` gains the branch above. Exact-text change of the left-value functions: a return that was reversed counts 0, so a `HELD_VALUE_REFUND` naming the earlier correction is then refused (nothing left, 55000); this reads only a state that no path can reach today (a correction's return is never reversed before this sub-slice), so no committed result changes.
      - Service: `RecordReturnReversalCorrectionCommand` (`settlement_id`, `earlier_correction_id`, `reason`, `evidence_reference`), `SettlementCorrectionService.record_return_reversal` and `confirm_return_reversal` (stale P5S02). Routes in 11e.

Unit tests for the command and the service. The 27 head-revision assertions move to `20261129_125`.

The recovery charge reads "paid from value a mistaken Refund return brought back" (the design said "paid from a Refund return recorded by mistake"). Seven tests in the new module (the four real paths, the refusals, the database guards with the Q-95 refusal, least privilege and forward-only); three unit tests. Rounds 1 and 2 (round 2: security clean; independent one text should-fix), round 3 under the standing rule. Open: Q-2026-10-12-94, Q-2026-10-12-95 (each refused until answered). Relaxing exact-text patches:
      - Q-94: `app.assert_settlement_return_reversal_allowed` drops the agreed-Signer refusal; the recovery scope check `app.settlement_return_reversal_refund` requires the Refund in the draft's recorded recoveries with the Refund's own recipient (instead of the Deposit's own Customer). The 10e-1c writer already charges the person who received the money: an `EXTRA_CHARGE` on the Lease when that person is its signed Customer, else a no-Lease charge.
      - Q-95: `app.settlement_return_reversal_source_state` adds the totals of the uses (`uses`: applied, refunded). When applied plus refunded is more than the returned amount and the Refunds paid out at least the returned amount, the returned amount is shared over the Refunds, the oldest first (owner answer to Q-2026-10-10-76: a new Refund pays the returned money first); each recovery entry carries its share as `amount_usd_cents` and the Refund's net as `net_usd_cents`; a Refund with no share is not recovered; the application lists are empty, so every application stays. Otherwise each Refund's whole net, as in 10e-3. The refusal of uses beyond the return is replaced by a narrower one: uses more than the returned amount while the Refunds paid out less than it (Q-2026-10-12-96, until answered; 55000, "The returned money also paid debt beyond what its Refunds paid out; reversing it waits for the owner").
      - Recovery writer `app.write_payment_return_recoveries`: an entry with `net_usd_cents` may recover less than the Refund's net: the planned net must still hold, and the amount is above 0 and at most the net; the charge and the `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row are for the amount, and the row's resulting net is the net less the amount. Entries without `net_usd_cents` (10e-1c, 10e-2) keep the whole-net rule.
      - General Refund correction check `app.validate_general_refund_correction` (DB-REFUND-002 amendment): a `RECOVERED_BY_CHARGE` row linked to a `RETURN_REVERSAL` correction recovers exactly the amount its draft recorded for that Refund (new internal `app.settlement_return_reversal_recovery_amount`, no grant), never more than the net; every other recovery still the whole net.

The 27 head-revision assertions move to `20261130_126` (owner standing rule).

The writer takes a share only inside a `RETURN_REVERSAL`. The source names `uses.returned_refunds`, and on the shared path a Refund of the uses with its own return is refused; missing totals are refused. The recovery-amount function also requires the confirmation in this transaction by the row's actor, and refuses a second recovery of the same Refund in one correction. A second internal function, `app.refund_recovery_source_reference`, numbers the charge reference of a later recovery (the recovery check is patched too), so the rest of a partly recovered Refund can be charged back later.
